FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/acceptance.mjs

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';import {spawn} from 'node:child_process';
const root=fileURLToPath(new URL('./',import.meta.url)),workspace=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../',import.meta.url));
const steps=[
 ['platform',['--test','--test-concurrency=1',path.join(root,'tests','*.test.mjs')]],
 ['browser',[path.join(root,'tests','browser-acceptance.mjs')]],
 ['public-browser',[path.join(root,'tests','public-browser-acceptance.mjs')]],
 ['private-browser',[path.join(root,'tests','private-browser-acceptance.mjs')]],
 ['community-browser',[path.join(root,'tests','community-browser-acceptance.mjs')]],
 ['market-browser',[path.join(root,'tests','market-browser-acceptance.mjs')]],
 ['book-browser',[path.join(root,'tests','book-browser-acceptance.mjs')]],
 ['backup',[path.join(root,'tests','backup-acceptance.mjs')]],
 ['legacy',[path.join(root,'tests','legacy-acceptance.mjs')]],
 ['existing-integrations',['--test',path.join(workspace,'deliveries','START_ADMIN_CONTENT_VISUAL_2026-10-07','map-proxy.test.mjs'),path.join(workspace,'deliveries','START_ADMIN_CONTENT_VISUAL_2026-10-07','extractor-api.test.mjs'),path.join(workspace,'deliveries','START_ADMIN_CONTENT_VISUAL_2026-10-07','batch-domain.test.cjs')]],
 ['local-smoke',[path.join(root,'tests','local-smoke.mjs')]],
 ['public-live-preview',[path.join(root,'tests','public-preview-smoke.mjs')]],
 ];const results=[];
for(const [name,args] of steps){const chunks=[];const code=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const p=spawn(process.execPath,args,{cwd:workspace,windowsHide:true});p.stdout.on('data',b=>chunks.push(b));p.stderr.on('data',b=>chunks.push(b));p.on('error',reject);p.on('exit',resolve);});await fs.writeFile(path.join(root,'evidence','acceptance-'+name+'.log'),Buffer.concat(chunks));results.push({name,passed:code===0,exitCode:code});await fs.writeFile(path.join(root,'evidence','ACCEPTANCE_RUN.json'),JSON.stringify({at:new Date().toISOString(),results,realWechat:false,realGps:false,cloudConnected:false,testDatabaseOnly:true},null,2));console.log((code===0?'PASS':'FAIL'),name);if(code!==0)process.exitCode=1;}
await fs.writeFile(path.join(root,'evidence','READINESS.json'),JSON.stringify({at:new Date().toISOString(),allAcceptanceGroupsPassed:results.length===steps.length&&results.every(r=>r.passed),acceptanceGroups:results.length,passedGroups:results.filter(r=>r.passed).length,failedGroups:results.filter(r=>!r.passed).map(r=>r.name),evidence:'ACCEPTANCE_RUN.json',publicFrontendWired:true,allFrontendWired:false,deviceAcceptance:false,realWechat:false,realGps:false,cloudConnected:false,review:'Scoped local stability review; browser fixture identities are test-only, not production acceptance'},null,2));

FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/boot.mjs

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';import {execFileSync} from 'node:child_process';import {Pool} from './src/runtime.mjs';
const data=fileURLToPath(new URL('./data/pg/',import.meta.url)),bin=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../integration-runtime/postgresql-18.6/pgsql/bin/',import.meta.url));
await fs.access(path.join(data,'PG_VERSION'));
try{execFileSync(path.join(bin,'pg_ctl.exe'),['-D',data,'status'],{stdio:'ignore',windowsHide:true,timeout:10000});}catch{execFileSync(path.join(bin,'pg_ctl.exe'),['-D',data,'-l',fileURLToPath(new URL('./evidence/postgres.log',import.meta.url)),'-o','-h 127.0.0.1 -p 55435','-t','30','-w','start'],{stdio:'ignore',windowsHide:true,timeout:35000});}
const pool=new Pool({host:'127.0.0.1',port:55435,user:'start_local',database:'postgres'});try{const actual=(await pool.query("SELECT current_setting('data_directory') AS dir")).rows[0].dir;if(path.resolve(actual)!==path.resolve(data))throw Error('Port belongs to a different database; no migration executed');if(!(await pool.query('SELECT 1 FROM pg_database WHERE datname=$1',['start_acceptance'])).rowCount)await pool.query('CREATE DATABASE start_acceptance');}finally{await pool.end();}
await import('./server.mjs');

await fs.mkdir(root,{recursive:true});
let html=await fs.readFile(new URL('index.html',source),'utf8');
html=html.replace('START · 内容建设后台视觉稿','START · 本地内容建设后台').replace('后台视觉稿','本地后台').replace('示例内容 · 操作仅在本页演示，刷新后重置','本机数据库 · 登录能力尚未配置').replace('ADMIN_DESIGN.md','/api/status').replace('平台管理员 · 演示角色','身份等待验证').replace('<script src="admin.js"></script>','<script src="admin.js"></script><script src="bootstrap.js"></script>');

FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/client.mjs

// Existing approved views can consume this adapter; no embedded fixture identity.
export function createPlatformClient({base='',getBearer=async()=>null,fetchImpl=fetch}={}){
 async function request(path,{method='GET',value,key,raw,signal}={}){
  const token=await getBearer(),headers={};if(token)headers.authorization='Bearer '+token;
  if(key)headers['idempotency-key']=key;if(value!==undefined)headers['content-type']='application/json';if(raw)headers['content-type']='image/png';
  const r=await fetchImpl(base+path,{method,headers,body:raw??(value===undefined?undefined:JSON.stringify(value)),cache:'no-store',signal});
  const data=await r.json();if(!r.ok){const e=new Error(data.error||'REQUEST_FAILED');e.code=data.error;e.status=r.status;throw e;}return data;
 }
 return {command:(op,value,key=crypto.randomUUID())=>request('/api/commands/'+encodeURIComponent(op),{method:'POST',value,key}),public:(filters={})=>request('/api/public?'+new URLSearchParams(filters)),detail:(kind,id)=>request('/api/public/'+encodeURIComponent(kind)+'/'+encodeURIComponent(id)),admin:(section,id)=>request('/api/admin/'+encodeURIComponent(section)+(id?'?'+new URLSearchParams({id}):'')),me:section=>request('/api/me/'+encodeURIComponent(section)),recommend:city=>request('/api/recommend?'+new URLSearchParams({city})),share:token=>request('/api/share/'+encodeURIComponent(token)),upload:bytes=>request('/api/media',{method:'POST',raw:bytes}),
 extract:(value,signal)=>request('/api/extract/places',{method:'POST',value,signal}),
 async imageUrl(id){const token=await getBearer();const r=await fetchImpl(base+'/api/media/'+encodeURIComponent(id),{headers:token?{authorization:'Bearer '+token}:{},cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)throw Object.assign(Error('MEDIA_UNAVAILABLE'),{code:'MEDIA_UNAVAILABLE'});return URL.createObjectURL(await r.blob());},
 async uploadImage(file){if(!['image/png','image/jpeg','image/webp'].includes(file.type)||file.size>8*1024*1024)throw Object.assign(Error('INVALID_MEDIA'),{code:'INVALID_MEDIA'});const image=await createImageBitmap(file);try{if(image.width>4096||image.height>4096||image.width*image.height>16_000_000)throw Object.assign(Error('INVALID_MEDIA'),{code:'INVALID_MEDIA'});const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=image.width;canvas.height=image.height;canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(image,0,0);const blob=await new Promise(r=>canvas.toBlob(r,'image/png'));if(!blob||blob.size>5*1024*1024)throw Object.assign(Error('INVALID_MEDIA'),{code:'INVALID_MEDIA'});return request('/api/media',{method:'POST',raw:blob});}finally{image.close();}}
 };
}

FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/db/001_platform.sql
================================================================================
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS start_app;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.admins(user_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.content(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,kind text NOT NULL CHECK(kind IN ('artist','place','route','event')),version integer NOT NULL CHECK(version>0),draft jsonb NOT NULL,published jsonb,poi_id text UNIQUE,updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now());
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.revisions(content_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.content(id),version integer NOT NULL,data jsonb NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(content_id,version));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.submissions(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),artist_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.content(id),data jsonb NOT NULL,status text NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('pending','approved','rejected')),reason text,place_id uuid REFERENCES start_app.content(id));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.contributions(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,submission_id uuid UNIQUE NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.submissions(id),place_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.content(id),artist_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.content(id),data jsonb NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.community_applications(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),artist_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.content(id),data jsonb NOT NULL,status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',reason text,community_id uuid);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.communities(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,artist_id uuid UNIQUE NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.content(id),manager_id uuid REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),name text NOT NULL,description text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',notice text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active');
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.memberships(community_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.communities(id),user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),status text NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('joined','followed','removed')),PRIMARY KEY(community_id,user_id));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.community_requests(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,community_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.communities(id),user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),data jsonb NOT NULL,status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending');
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.listings(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),community_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.communities(id),artist_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.content(id),event_id uuid REFERENCES start_app.content(id),place_id uuid REFERENCES start_app.content(id),data jsonb NOT NULL,status text NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('available','withdrawn')));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.exchanges(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,listing_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.listings(id),requester_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),status text NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('pending','accepted','withdrawn')),UNIQUE(listing_id,requester_id));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.contacts(exchange_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.exchanges(id),user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),value text NOT NULL CHECK(length(value) BETWEEN 1 AND 200),PRIMARY KEY(exchange_id,user_id));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.checkins(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),place_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.content(id),at timestamptz NOT NULL,snapshot jsonb NOT NULL,text text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',media_ids uuid[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',ticket jsonb);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.shares(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),token_hash text UNIQUE NOT NULL,record_ids uuid[] NOT NULL,fields text[] NOT NULL,revoked boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.dolls(user_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),character text NOT NULL CHECK(character IN ('boy','girl')),outfit text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'original',expression text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'neutral');
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.media(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),mime text NOT NULL,size integer NOT NULL,sha256 text NOT NULL,visibility text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'private' CHECK(visibility IN ('private','public')),path text NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.favorites(user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),content_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.content(id),PRIMARY KEY(user_id,content_id));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.notifications(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),kind text NOT NULL,reference_id uuid NOT NULL,read boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now());
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.audit(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),operation text NOT NULL,reference_id uuid,created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now());
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.idempotency(user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),operation text NOT NULL,key text NOT NULL,fingerprint text NOT NULL,result jsonb NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(user_id,operation,key));
ALTER TABLE start_app.submissions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS kind text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'place';
ALTER TABLE start_app.submissions ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS content_id uuid REFERENCES start_app.content(id);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.corrections(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),content_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.content(id),data jsonb NOT NULL,status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',reason text);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.settings(key text PRIMARY KEY,data jsonb NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS start_app.listing_comments(id uuid PRIMARY KEY,listing_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_app.listings(id),user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES start_accounts.users(id),text text NOT NULL CHECK(length(text) BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now());
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS listing_comments_listing ON start_app.listing_comments(listing_id,created_at,id);
REVOKE ALL ON SCHEMA start_app FROM PUBLIC;
REVOKE ALL ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA start_app FROM PUBLIC;

FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/public-ui/book.mjs
================================================================================
// Uses the approved ticket classes and the existing private form vocabulary.
const esc=value=>String(value??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const drafts=new Map(),listPosition={y:0};
const fields=[['snapshot','地点'],['at','打卡时间'],['text','记录文字'],['ticket','纪念票根'],['media_ids','照片']];
const date=value=>value?new Date(value).toLocaleString('zh-CN',{hour12:false}):'';
const target=id=>'#/book/'+encodeURIComponent(id);
const link=(href,text)=>`<a class="inline-link" href="${href}">${text}</a>`;
const photos=r=>(r.media_ids||[]).map((id,i)=>`<span class="book-photo-slot" data-book-photo="${esc(id)}" data-photo-name="${i+1}">正在载入照片…</span>`).join('');

function ticket(r){
 if(!r.ticket)return '';
 let teeth='';for(let x=280;x>=20;x-=10)teeth+=` L${x-3} 708 L${x-8} 703`;
 return `<div class="approved-ticket-wrap"><div class="ticket-cord" aria-hidden="true"></div><article class="memory-ticket ticket-long" aria-label="纪念票根"><svg class="ticket-outline" viewBox="0 0 300 708" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M22 0 H278 Q278 22 300 22 V132 Q289 136 300 140 V518 Q289 522 300 526 V695 Q282 695 280 703 ${teeth} L20 703 Q18 695 0 695 V526 Q11 522 0 518 V140 Q11 136 0 132 V22 Q22 22 22 0 Z"/></svg><div class="ticket-hole" aria-hidden="true"></div><header class="ticket-mast"><div><div class="ticket-logo"><img src="/app/assets/START_LOGO_APPROVED.png" alt="START"></div><span class="ticket-brandline">A LITTLE JOURNEY, KEPT.</span></div><span class="ticket-issue">TRAVEL / 01</span></header><div class="ticket-meta"><div>PLACES<br>MOMENTS<br>LITTLE DETOURS</div><div>${esc(r.at?date(r.at):'')}<br>${esc(r.snapshot?.city||'')}<span class="ticket-count">${esc(r.snapshot?.name||'')}</span></div></div><section class="ticket-poster"><div class="poster-shapes" aria-hidden="true"><span></span><span></span><span></span><span></span></div><p class="ticket-edition">START TRAVEL JOURNAL</p><h2>${esc(r.ticket.title)}</h2><p class="ticket-note">${esc(r.ticket.note||'')}</p></section><section class="ticket-summary"><div><span>EDITION</span><strong>城市漫游</strong></div><div><span>CHAPTER</span><strong>${esc(r.snapshot?.city||'记录')}</strong></div><div><span>START BOOK</span><strong>纪念票根</strong></div></section><footer class="ticket-stub"><div class="ticket-barcode"><div class="decorative-barcode" aria-hidden="true"></div><small>装饰条码 · 不可扫码</small></div><span class="ticket-stub-note">KEEP<br>THIS<br>MOMENT<b>↘</b></span></footer></article></div>`;
}
function recordMarkup(r){
 return `<article class="book-record">${r.snapshot?`<h2>${esc(r.snapshot.name)}</h2><p class="public-badge">${esc([r.snapshot.city,r.snapshot.category].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</p>`:''}${r.at?`<p>${esc(date(r.at))}</p>`:''}${r.text?`<p class="book-text">${esc(r.text)}</p>`:''}<div class="book-photos">${photos(r)}</div>${ticket(r)}</article>`;
}
function newForm(placeId){
 const localNow=new Date(Date.now()-new Date().getTimezoneOffset()*60000).toISOString().slice(0,16);
 const wrapper=document.createElement('div');
 wrapper.innerHTML=`<form data-book-form="checkin"><label for="book-at">打卡时间</label><input id="book-at" name="at" type="datetime-local" required value="${localNow}"><label for="book-text">记录文字</label><textarea id="book-text" name="text" maxlength="3500" rows="4"></textarea><label for="book-media">照片（最多 9 张）</label><input id="book-media" name="media" type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" multiple><label for="book-title">票根标题</label><input id="book-title" name="title" maxlength="200"><label for="book-note">票根短句</label><textarea id="book-note" name="note" maxlength="1000" rows="2"></textarea><button type="submit" class="primary">保存到 Book</button></form>`;
 const form=wrapper.firstElementChild;
 form.bookState={placeId,uploads:new WeakMap(),retry:new Map(),pending:false};return form;
}
export function mountBook(root,api,{path,placeId='',catalog}){
 const host=root.querySelector('[data-book-workspace]');let alive=true,busy=false,records=[],shares=[],preview=null;
 const urls=new Set();
 const status=text=>{if(alive){const el=host.querySelector('[data-book-status]');el.textContent=text;}};
 const shell=html=>{if(!alive)return;for(const url of urls)URL.revokeObjectURL(url);urls.clear();host.innerHTML=`<p data-book-status role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>${html}`;};
 async function hydrate(token){
  for(const slot of host.querySelectorAll('[data-book-photo]')){
   let url;
   try{
    url=token?'/api/share/'+encodeURIComponent(token)+'/media/'+encodeURIComponent(slot.dataset.bookPhoto):await api.imageUrl(slot.dataset.bookPhoto);
    if(!alive||!slot.isConnected){if(!token)URL.revokeObjectURL(url);continue;}
    if(!token)urls.add(url);
    const img=document.createElement('img');img.alt=(token?'分享照片 ':'打卡照片 ')+slot.dataset.photoName;
    img.onload=()=>{if(alive&&slot.isConnected)slot.replaceWith(img);};
    img.onerror=()=>{if(alive&&slot.isConnected)slot.textContent='照片暂不可用';};img.src=url;
   }catch{if(alive&&slot.isConnected)slot.textContent='照片暂不可用';}
  }
 }
 async function command(op,value,retry){
  const fingerprint=op+JSON.stringify(value);let key=retry.get(fingerprint);
  if(!key){key=crypto.randomUUID();retry.set(fingerprint,key);}
  return api.command(op,value,key);
 }
 const mutationKeys=new Map();
 async function load(){
  if(path.startsWith('/share/')){
   const token=path.slice(7);
   try{const selected=await api.share(token);shell('<h1>分享的记录</h1>'+selected.map(recordMarkup).join(''));hydrate(token);}
   catch(e){shell(`<h1>${e.status===404?'分享已失效':'暂时无法载入分享'}</h1><p>${e.status===404?'链接已撤销或记录已删除。':'请稍后重试。'}</p>`);}return;
  }
  try{
   records=(await api.me('book')).sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(b.at)-Date.parse(a.at)||b.id.localeCompare(a.id));
   if(path==='/book/shares')shares=await api.me('shares');
   if(!alive)return;
   if(path==='/book/new'){
    const place=catalog.places.find(p=>p.id===placeId);
    if(!place){shell('<h1>地点已撤回或不存在</h1>'+link('#/discover','选择地点'));return;}
    shell(`<h1>记录打卡</h1><h2>${esc(place.name)}</h2><p class="public-badge">仅自己可见</p><div data-book-form-host></div>`);
    let form=drafts.get(placeId);if(!form){form=newForm(placeId);drafts.set(placeId,form);}
    host.querySelector('[data-book-form-host]').append(form);
    if(form.bookState.pending){
     busy=true;form.querySelector('[type=submit]').disabled=true;status('正在保存，请稍候…');
     form.bookState.task.then(savedId=>{if(!alive)return;busy=false;form.querySelector('[type=submit]').disabled=false;if(savedId)location.hash=target(savedId);else status(form.bookState.error);});
    }
   }else if(path==='/book/share'){
    shell(`<h1>分享所选内容</h1><form data-book-form="share"><fieldset><legend>选择记录（最多 30 条）</legend>${records.map(r=>`<label class="book-choice"><input type="checkbox" name="ids" value="${r.id}">选择记录：${esc(r.snapshot.name)}</label>`).join('')||'<p>还没有打卡记录</p>'}</fieldset><fieldset><legend>公开内容</legend>${fields.map(([id,name])=>`<label class="book-choice"><input type="checkbox" name="fields" value="${id}" ${['snapshot','at'].includes(id)?'checked':''}>${name}</label>`).join('')}</fieldset><button type="submit" class="primary">预览分享</button></form><div data-book-preview></div>`);
   }else if(path==='/book/shares'){
    shell(`<h1>分享管理</h1><p class="public-badge">可撤销已有链接；完整链接仅在创建时显示。</p>${shares.map(s=>`<section class="private-section"><h2>${s.record_ids.length} 条记录</h2><p>${s.fields.map(f=>esc(fields.find(x=>x[0]===f)?.[1]||f)).join(' · ')}</p>${s.revoked?'<span class="status-pill">已撤销</span>':`<button type="button" data-book-action="revoke" data-id="${s.id}">撤销链接</button>`}</section>`).join('')||'<p>还没有分享链接</p>'}`);
   }else if(path==='/book'){
    shell(`<h1>START Book</h1><p class="public-badge">仅自己可见</p><div class="private-actions">${link('#/book/share','分享所选内容')}${link('#/book/shares','分享管理')}</div>${records.map(r=>`<a class="review-place-row" href="${target(r.id)}"><span class="place-image">${esc(r.snapshot.city)}</span><span><strong>${esc(r.snapshot.name)}</strong><small>${esc(date(r.at))}</small></span><span aria-hidden="true">›</span></a>`).join('')||'<p>还没有打卡记录</p>'+link('#/discover','去发现地点')}`);
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(alive)scrollTo(0,listPosition.y);});
   }else{
    const r=records.find(r=>r.id===path.slice(6));
    if(!r){shell('<h1>记录不存在或已删除</h1>');return;}
    shell(`<h1>打卡记录</h1>${recordMarkup(r)}<div class="private-actions">${link('#/book/share','分享所选内容')}<button type="button" data-book-action="delete" data-id="${r.id}">删除记录</button></div><dialog class="book-confirm"><h2>删除这条记录？</h2><p>相关分享链接也会失效。</p><button type="button" data-book-action="cancel">取消</button><button type="button" data-book-action="confirm-delete" data-id="${r.id}">确认删除</button></dialog>`);hydrate();
   }
  }catch(e){
   if(['AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED','UNAUTHENTICATED','UNAUTHORIZED'].includes(e.code))shell('<h1>START Book</h1><p>登录后可以查看私人记录。</p>');
   else shell('<h1>暂时无法载入记录</h1><button type="button" data-book-action="retry">重试</button>');
  }
 }
 async function submit(e){
  const form=e.target.closest('[data-book-form]');if(!form)return;e.preventDefault();if(busy||!alive)return;
  const data=new FormData(form);
  if(form.dataset.bookForm==='share'){
   const ids=data.getAll('ids'),selected=data.getAll('fields');
   if(!ids.length||ids.length>30||!selected.length){status('请选择 1–30 条记录和至少一种公开内容。');return;}
   preview={ids,fields:selected};
   host.querySelector('[data-book-preview]').innerHTML=`<section role="region" aria-label="分享预览"><h2>分享预览</h2>${ids.map(id=>{const r=records.find(r=>r.id===id);return recordMarkup(Object.fromEntries(selected.map(f=>[f,r[f]])));}).join('')}<button type="button" data-book-action="create-share" class="primary">创建分享链接</button></section>`;hydrate();return;
  }
  const state=form.bookState,files=data.getAll('media').filter(file=>file.size),at=new Date(data.get('at'));
  if(!Number.isFinite(at.getTime())||at.getTime()>Date.now()){status('请选择现在或过去的打卡时间。');return;}
  if(files.length>9){status('照片最多 9 张。');return;}
  if(!String(data.get('title')).trim()&&String(data.get('note')).trim()){status('请为票根填写标题。');return;}
  busy=true;state.pending=true;state.savedId=null;state.task=new Promise(resolve=>{state.finish=resolve;});const button=form.querySelector('[type=submit]');button.disabled=true;status('正在保存…');
  try{
   const mediaIds=[];for(const file of files){let id=state.uploads.get(file);if(!id){id=(await api.uploadImage(file)).id;state.uploads.set(file,id);}mediaIds.push(id);}
   const value={placeId:state.placeId,at:at.toISOString(),text:String(data.get('text')),mediaIds,...(String(data.get('title')).trim()?{ticket:{title:String(data.get('title')).trim(),note:String(data.get('note'))}}:{})};
   const saved=await command('checkin.create',value,state.retry);state.savedId=saved.id;drafts.delete(state.placeId);
   if(alive)location.hash=target(saved.id);
  }catch(e){state.error=e.code==='AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED'?'登录服务尚未接入，暂时不能保存。':e.code==='INVALID_MEDIA'?'照片格式或大小不符合要求，请重新选择。':'保存未完成，内容已保留，请重试。';status(state.error);}
  finally{state.pending=false;busy=false;button.disabled=false;state.finish(state.savedId);}
 }
 async function click(e){
  const b=e.target.closest('[data-book-action]');if(!b||!alive||busy)return;
  const action=b.dataset.bookAction,id=b.dataset.id;
  if(action==='retry'){load();return;}
  if(action==='delete'){host.querySelector('dialog').showModal();return;}
  if(action==='cancel'){host.querySelector('dialog').close();return;}
  busy=true;b.disabled=true;
  try{
   if(action==='create-share'){
    if(!preview)return;const result=await command('share.create',preview,mutationKeys);
    if(!alive)return;
    const url=location.origin+'/app/#/share/'+encodeURIComponent(result.token);
    host.querySelector('[data-book-preview]').innerHTML=`<h2>链接已创建</h2><label for="book-link">分享链接</label><input id="book-link" value="${esc(url)}" readonly><p class="public-badge">当前为本地链接，只用于本机预览。</p>${link('#/share/'+encodeURIComponent(result.token),'打开分享页')}${link('#/book/shares','分享管理')}`;
   }else if(action==='revoke'){await command('share.revoke',{id},mutationKeys);await load();status('链接已撤销。');}
   else if(action==='confirm-delete'){await command('checkin.delete',{id},mutationKeys);if(alive)location.hash='#/book';}
  }catch{status('操作未完成，请重试。');}
  finally{busy=false;b.disabled=false;}
 }
 function change(e){if(e.target.closest('[data-book-form=share]')){preview=null;host.querySelector('[data-book-preview]').replaceChildren();}}
 const remember=()=>{if(path==='/book')listPosition.y=scrollY;};
 host.addEventListener('submit',submit);host.addEventListener('click',click);host.addEventListener('change',change);host.addEventListener('click',remember);load();
 return()=>{alive=false;host.removeEventListener('submit',submit);host.removeEventListener('click',click);host.removeEventListener('change',change);host.removeEventListener('click',remember);for(const url of urls)URL.revokeObjectURL(url);urls.clear();};
}

// Pending writes survive route changes, while views always re-read account state.
const pending=new Map();

export function mountCommunityMembership(root,catalog,api,communityId){
 const section=document.createElement('section');section.className='private-section membership-section';
 section.setAttribute('aria-label',communityId?'社群成员状态':'我的社群');
 root.querySelector('h1').after(section);
 if(!communityId){const heading=document.createElement('h2');heading.textContent='我的社群';section.append(heading);}
 const body=document.createElement('div');section.append(body);
 const feedback=document.createElement('p');feedback.setAttribute('role','status');feedback.className='public-badge';section.append(feedback);
 let disposed=false,revision=0,memberships=[];
 function message(text){feedback.textContent=text;}
 function button(text,action){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='secondary';b.textContent=text;b.onclick=action;body.append(b);return b;}
 function showError(error,read){
  body.replaceChildren();
  const auth=error.code==='AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED'?'身份服务尚未接入，暂时不能加入社群。':error.status===401?'登录后可以加入社群。':error.status===403?'当前账号不能操作此社群。':error.status===404?'社群已关闭或不存在。':'';
  message(auth||(read?'暂时无法读取成员状态。':'操作未成功，请重试。'));
  if(!auth)button('重试成员状态',load);
 }
 function paint(){
  body.replaceChildren();
  if(!communityId){
   const joined=memberships.filter(m=>m.status==='joined').map(m=>catalog.communities.find(c=>c.id===m.community_id)).filter(Boolean);
   for(const community of joined){const a=document.createElement('a');a.className='inline-link';a.href='#/community/'+encodeURIComponent(community.id);a.textContent=community.name+' ›';body.append(a);}
   if(!joined.length)message('还没有加入社群');else message('');
   return;
  }
  const joined=memberships.some(m=>m.community_id===communityId&&m.status==='joined');
  const b=button(joined?'退出社群':'加入社群',()=>change(joined?'removed':'joined',b));
  b.dataset.membershipAction='';b.className=joined?'secondary':'primary';
 }
 async function load(){
  const current=++revision;body.replaceChildren();message('正在读取成员状态…');
  // A navigation during a write must not display its earlier server snapshot.
  await Promise.allSettled([...pending.values()]);
  if(disposed||current!==revision)return;
  try{
   const value=await api.me('memberships');if(disposed||current!==revision)return;
   if(!Array.isArray(value))throw Error('INVALID_RESPONSE');memberships=value;message('');paint();
  }catch(error){if(!disposed&&current===revision)showError(error,true);}
 }
 async function change(status,b){
  if(b.disabled||pending.has(communityId))return;
  b.disabled=true;section.dataset.membershipBusy='true';message(status==='joined'?'正在加入…':'正在退出…');
  const operation=api.command('community.join',{id:communityId,status});pending.set(communityId,operation);
  try{
   const result=await operation;
   if(disposed)return;
   if(result.id!==communityId||result.status!==status)throw Error('INVALID_RESPONSE');
   memberships=memberships.filter(m=>m.community_id!==communityId);
   if(status==='joined')memberships.push({community_id:communityId,status});
   paint();message(status==='joined'?'已加入社群。':'已退出社群。');body.querySelector('button')?.focus({preventScroll:true});
  }catch(error){
   if(disposed)return;
   if(error.code==='AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED'||[401,403,404].includes(error.status))showError(error,false);
   else{b.disabled=false;message('操作未成功，请重试。');}
  }finally{
   if(pending.get(communityId)===operation)pending.delete(communityId);
   delete section.dataset.membershipBusy;
  }
 }
 load();return ()=>{disposed=true;revision++;section.remove();};
}

// A separate, explicitly labelled visual prototype. Never changes original assets or adds an identity.
const badge=document.createElement('aside');badge.setAttribute('aria-label','视觉原型');badge.innerHTML='<a>视觉原型 · 返回数据前台</a>';badge.querySelector('a').href=location.origin+'/app/#/me';Object.assign(badge.style,{position:'fixed',bottom:'78px',right:'12px',zIndex:'100',padding:'8px 12px',background:'#fbf3e8',border:'1px solid #d7beb0',borderRadius:'24px',font:'12px system-ui'});document.body.append(badge);
function normalize(){document.querySelectorAll('#app a[href^="/"],#sheet a[href^="/"]').forEach(a=>{const path=a.getAttribute('href');a.href=location.origin+(path==='/'||path==='/community'?'/app/#'+path:'/app/legacy-me.html#'+path);});}
const observer=new MutationObserver(normalize);observer.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});normalize();
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const button=e.target.closest('[data-href]');if(button&&['/','/community'].includes(button.dataset.href)){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();location.href='/app/#'+button.dataset.href;}},true);

const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const button=(op,id,label)=>`<button type="button" data-market-action="${op}" data-id="${esc(id)}">${label}</button>`;
export function mountMarket(root,catalog,api,listingId){
 let alive=true,busy=false,own=[],exchanges=[],comments=[],memberships=[];
 const host=root.querySelector('[data-market-workspace]');
 const message=text=>{if(alive)host.querySelector('[role=status]').textContent=text;};
 const error=e=>['AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED','UNAUTHENTICATED'].includes(e.code)?'登录服务尚未接入，暂时不能操作。':'操作未完成，请重试；填写内容已保留。';
 async function load(){
  let item=listingId?await api.detail('listing',listingId):null,authenticated=true;
  try{[own,exchanges,comments,memberships]=await Promise.all(['listings','exchanges','listingComments','memberships'].map(s=>api.me(s)));}catch(e){if(!['AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED','UNAUTHENTICATED','UNAUTHORIZED'].includes(e.code))throw e;authenticated=false;own=[];exchanges=[];comments=[];memberships=[];}
  if(!alive)return;
  const mine=own.find(l=>l.id===listingId);
  host.innerHTML=`<p role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>${!authenticated?'<p>登录服务尚未接入，可以查看物料和评论。</p>':''}`;
  if(item){host.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<h1>${esc(item.name)}</h1><p>${esc(item.description)}</p>${(item.mediaIds||[]).map(id=>`<img class="hero-media" src="/api/media/${esc(id)}" alt="物料照片">`).join('')}<div class="private-actions">${authenticated&&!mine&&!exchanges.some(e=>e.listing_id===listingId)?button('exchange.apply',listingId,'申请交换'):''}<a href="#/market">我的物料与申请</a></div><h2>评论</h2>${(item.comments||[]).map(c=>`<article class="private-row"><div><strong>${esc(c.author||'用户')}</strong><p>${esc(c.text)}</p></div>${comments.some(x=>x.id===c.id)?button('listing.comment.delete',c.id,'删除'):''}</article>`).join('')||'<p>还没有评论</p>'}${authenticated?'<form data-market-form="comment"><label for="market-comment">评论公开可见，联系方式由你自行决定是否填写</label><textarea id="market-comment" name="text" required maxlength="1000"></textarea><button type="submit">发布评论</button></form>':''}`);}
  else if(authenticated){
   host.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<h1>我的物料与申请</h1><h2>我的物料</h2>${own.map(l=>`<article class="private-section"><h3>${esc(l.data.name)}</h3><span class="status-pill">${l.status==='available'?'可交换':'已撤下'}</span><div class="private-actions">${l.status==='available'?`<a href="#/listing/${l.id}">查看评论</a>`:''}${button('edit',l.id,'编辑')}${button(l.status==='available'?'withdraw':'restore',l.id,l.status==='available'?'撤下':'重新上架')}</div></article>`).join('')||'<p>还没有发布物料</p>'}<h2>交换申请</h2>${exchanges.map(e=>`<article class="private-section"><strong>${esc(e.name)}</strong><p>${e.publisher?'收到的申请':'我的申请'} · ${esc({pending:'待接受',accepted:'已接受',withdrawn:'已撤回'}[e.status])}</p><div class="private-actions">${e.publisher&&e.status==='pending'?button('exchange.accept',e.id,'接受申请'):''}${e.status!=='withdrawn'?button('exchange.withdraw',e.id,'撤回申请'):''}</div></article>`).join('')||'<p>暂无交换申请</p>'}<h2>发布物料</h2><div data-market-editor></div>`);editor();
  }
 }
 function editor(id){
  const l=own.find(x=>x.id===id),joined=catalog.communities.filter(c=>memberships.some(m=>m.community_id===c.id&&m.status==='joined'));
  host.querySelector('[data-market-editor]').innerHTML=`<form data-market-form="listing" data-id="${esc(id||'')}">${l?'':`<label for="market-community">社群</label><select id="market-community" name="communityId" required><option value="">选择已加入的社群</option>${joined.map(c=>`<option value="${c.id}">${esc(c.name)}</option>`).join('')}</select><label for="market-event">关联演出（选填）</label><select id="market-event" name="eventId"><option value="">不关联演出</option></select>`}<label for="market-name">物料名称</label><input id="market-name" name="name" required maxlength="200" value="${esc(l?.data.name||'')}"><label for="market-description">物料说明</label><textarea id="market-description" name="description" maxlength="3500">${esc(l?.data.description||'')}</textarea><label for="market-photo">照片（最多 9 张；编辑时选择照片将替换原照片）</label><input id="market-photo" type="file" name="media" accept="image/*" multiple><button type="submit">${l?'保存修改':'发布物料'}</button></form>`;
 }
 async function click(e){const b=e.target.closest('[data-market-action]');if(!b||busy)return;const op=b.dataset.marketAction,id=b.dataset.id;if(op==='edit'){editor(id);host.querySelector('[data-market-editor]').scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});return;}busy=true;b.disabled=true;try{await api.command(op==='withdraw'||op==='restore'?'listing.status':op,op==='exchange.apply'?{listingId:id}:op==='withdraw'||op==='restore'?{id,status:op==='withdraw'?'withdrawn':'available'}:{id});await load();message('已更新。');}catch(e){message(error(e));}finally{busy=false;b.disabled=false;}}
 async function submit(e){const f=e.target.closest('[data-market-form]');if(!f)return;e.preventDefault();if(busy)return;busy=true;const b=f.querySelector('[type=submit]');b.disabled=true;try{const data=new FormData(f);if(f.dataset.marketForm==='comment')await api.command('listing.comment',{listingId,text:String(data.get('text'))});else{const files=data.getAll('media').filter(f=>f.size);if(files.length>9)throw Error('too many images');const mediaIds=[];for(const file of files)mediaIds.push((await api.uploadImage(file)).id);const value={name:String(data.get('name')).trim(),description:String(data.get('description')), ...(mediaIds.length?{mediaIds}:{})};if(f.dataset.id)await api.command('listing.edit',{id:f.dataset.id,...value});else{const community=catalog.communities.find(c=>c.id===data.get('communityId'));if(!community)throw Error('community missing');await api.command('listing.create',{...value,communityId:community.id,artistId:community.artist_id,status:'available',...(data.get('eventId')?{eventId:String(data.get('eventId'))}:{})});}}await load();message('已保存。');}catch(e){message(error(e));}finally{busy=false;b.disabled=false;}}
 function change(e){if(e.target.name!=='communityId')return;const c=catalog.communities.find(c=>c.id===e.target.value);host.querySelector('[name=eventId]').innerHTML='<option value="">不关联演出</option>'+catalog.events.filter(v=>v.artistIds.includes(c?.artist_id)).map(v=>`<option value="${v.id}">${esc(v.name)}</option>`).join('');}
 host.addEventListener('click',click);host.addEventListener('submit',submit);host.addEventListener('change',change);load().catch(e=>{if(alive)host.innerHTML='<p role="status">物料已撤下或暂时无法载入。</p>';});
 return()=>{alive=false;host.removeEventListener('click',click);host.removeEventListener('submit',submit);host.removeEventListener('change',change);};
}

const esc = value => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, character => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[character]));
const empty = text => `<p class="empty">${esc(text)}</p>`;
const statusLabel = status => ({pending:'待审核',approved:'已通过',rejected:'已退回',joined:'已加入',followed:'已关注',available:'可交换',withdrawn:'已撤回',accepted:'已接受'}[status] || status || '未设置');
const section = (title, body) => `<section class="private-section"><div class="section-head"><h2>${esc(title)}</h2></div>${body}</section>`;
const row = (title, meta, status) => `<div class="private-row"><div><strong>${esc(title)}</strong><small>${esc(meta || '')}</small></div>${status ? `<span class="status-pill">${esc(statusLabel(status))}</span>` : ''}</div>`;

export function renderPrivateMe({authError = '', profile = null, memberships = [], notifications = [], submissions = [], exchanges = [], listings = [], book = [], doll = null} = {}) {
  const profileBlock = profile
    ? `<div class="private-profile"><strong>${esc(profile.nickname || 'START 用户')}</strong><span>${esc(profile.artists || '还没有添加关注艺人')}</span><span>${esc(profile.interests || '还没有设置兴趣')}</span></div>`
    : empty(authError ? '登录后显示昵称、关注艺人和兴趣' : '暂无个人资料');
  const authBlock = authError
    ? `<div class="auth-note" role="status"><strong>身份服务尚未接入</strong><p>本地原型保留完整入口；接入微信身份后，投稿、收藏、打卡和交换申请会写入你的账号。</p></div>`
    : '';
  const joinedMemberships=memberships.filter(item=>item.status==='joined');
  const membershipBlock = joinedMemberships.length ? joinedMemberships.map(item => `<a class="inline-link" href="#/community/${encodeURIComponent(item.community_id)}">${esc(item.name || item.artistName || '社群')} ›</a>`).join('') : empty('还没有加入社群');
  const submissionBlock = submissions.length ? submissions.map(item => row(item.data?.name || item.name || '未命名投稿', item.data?.city || item.kind || '投稿', item.status)).join('') : empty('还没有投稿地点或路线');
  const exchangeBlock = exchanges.length ? exchanges.map(item => row(item.name || `物料申请 ${String(item.id || '').slice(0, 8)}`, '线下交换由双方自行确认', item.status)).join('') : empty('还没有物料交换申请');
  const listingBlock = listings.length ? listings.map(item => row(item.data?.name || item.name || '我的物料', '物料集市', item.status)).join('') : empty('还没有发布物料');
  const bookBlock = book.length ? book.map(item => row(item.snapshot?.name || item.text || 'START Book 记录', item.at ? new Date(item.at).toLocaleDateString('zh-CN') : '私人记录')).join('') : empty('还没有私人打卡记录');
  const notificationBlock = notifications.length ? notifications.map(item => row(item.kind || '新通知', item.created_at ? new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleString('zh-CN') : '', item.read ? '已读' : '未读')).join('') : empty('暂无通知');
  const dollBlock = doll ? `<div class="private-doll"><strong>${esc(doll.character === 'boy' ? '男娃' : '女娃')}</strong><span>${esc(doll.outfit || 'original')} · ${esc(doll.expression || 'neutral')}</span></div>` : empty('首次进入时选择一个娃娃');
  return `<div class="private-shell">
    <div class="public-top"><a href="#/">‹ 首页</a><span class="public-badge">ME</span></div>
    <header class="private-hero"><p class="public-badge">START · 私人空间</p><h1>我的工作台</h1><p>把走过的地点、喜欢的艺人和想交换的物料放在一起。</p></header>
    ${authBlock}
    <a class="inline-link" href="/app/legacy-me.html#/me">查看已确认的 Me 视觉原型</a>
    ${section('个人资料', profileBlock)}
    <section class="private-actions" aria-label="提交地点或路线"><p class="public-badge">提交地点或路线</p><a class="primary" href="#/submit?kind=place">提交地点</a><a class="primary" href="#/submit?kind=route">提交路线</a><a class="secondary" href="#/community">打开社群</a></section>
    ${section('我的社群', membershipBlock)}
    ${section('我的投稿', submissionBlock)}
    ${section('物料交换', `<div class="private-actions"><a href="#/market">管理物料与申请</a></div>${listingBlock}${exchangeBlock}`)}
    ${section('START Book', `<div class="private-actions"><a href="#/book">打开 START Book</a></div>${bookBlock}`)}
    ${section('我的娃娃', `${dollBlock}<a class="inline-link" href="/app/legacy-me.html#/me/doll">换装与表情 ›</a>`)}
    ${section('通知', notificationBlock)}
  </div>`;
}

export function renderSubmissionForm({kind = 'place', artists = []} = {}) {
  const route = kind === 'route';
  const artistOptions = artists.length ? artists.map(artist => `<option value="${esc(artist.id)}">${esc(artist.name)}</option>`).join('') : '<option value="">暂无艺人</option>';
  const categoryOptions = ['美食','咖啡','娱乐','景点','书店','其他'].map(category => `<option value="${category}">${category}</option>`).join('');
  return `<div class="submission-shell">
    <div class="public-top"><a href="#/me">‹ 我的工作台</a><span class="public-badge">投稿</span></div>
    <header class="view-title"><p class="public-badge">COMMUNITY · 内容投稿</p><h1>${route ? '提交路线' : '提交地点'}</h1><p>${route ? '把一组已核对的打卡点整理成一条路线。' : '把艺人打卡点补充到对应城市和分类里。'}</p></header>
    <div class="submission-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="投稿类型"><a role="tab" aria-selected="${!route}" href="#/submit?kind=place">地点</a><a role="tab" aria-selected="${route}" href="#/submit?kind=route">路线</a></div>
    <form data-private-submit data-kind="${route ? 'route' : 'place'}" novalidate>
      <div class="form-status" data-form-status role="alert" tabindex="-1" hidden></div>
      <label for="submission-name">名称<span aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><input id="submission-name" name="name" required maxlength="200" autocomplete="off">
      <label for="submission-city">城市<span aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><input id="submission-city" name="city" required maxlength="200" autocomplete="address-level2">
      <label for="submission-artist">艺人<span aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><select id="submission-artist" name="artistId" required>${artistOptions}</select>
      ${route ? `<p id="submission-stops-label">站点<span aria-hidden="true">*</span></p><ol class="route-stop-list" data-route-stops aria-label="路线站点"></ol>` : `<label for="submission-category">类别<span aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><select id="submission-category" name="category" required>${categoryOptions}</select><label for="submission-same-spot">打卡同款</label><textarea id="submission-same-spot" name="sameSpot" rows="3" maxlength="3500" placeholder="例如：MV 00:36 的取景位置"></textarea>`}
      <label for="submission-source">来源<span aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><textarea id="submission-source" name="source" required rows="3" maxlength="3500" placeholder="原帖、视频或现场记录来源"></textarea>
      <label for="submission-description">补充说明</label><textarea id="submission-description" name="description" rows="3" maxlength="3500"></textarea>
      <div class="submission-tools"><label for="map-search">在地图里找地点</label><div class="tool-row"><input id="map-search" name="mapQuery" placeholder="搜索地点名称"><button type="button" class="secondary" data-map-search>地图搜索</button></div><small>选择高德搜索结果后回填地点与坐标，请核对实际位置。</small></div>
      <div class="submission-tools"><label for="text-import">从文字识别</label><textarea id="text-import" name="textImport" rows="4" maxlength="3500" placeholder="粘贴小红书或其他来源的地点文字"></textarea><button type="button" class="secondary" data-text-import>识别文字</button><small>识别结果会填回表单，提交前请逐项检查。</small></div>
      <label for="submission-media">现场照片</label><input id="submission-media" name="media" type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" multiple>
      <button class="primary submit-button" type="submit">提交审核</button>
    </form>
  </div>`;
}

import {mountBook} from './book.mjs';
import {mountMarket} from './market.mjs';

document.querySelector('.skip-link').addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();document.querySelector('#app').focus();});
const api=createPlatformClient(),root=document.querySelector('#app'),esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c])),art='assets/HOME_APPROVED.png',categories=['美食','咖啡','娱乐','景点','书店','其他'];
let generation=0,cleanup=()=>{},catalog=null;
const state=()=>{const u=new URL((location.hash.slice(1)||'/'),'http://local');return {path:u.pathname,q:u.searchParams.get('q')||'',city:u.searchParams.get('city')||'',artistId:u.searchParams.get('artistId')||'',category:u.searchParams.get('category')||'',kind:u.searchParams.get('kind')||'place'};};
const link=(path,params={})=>'#'+path+(Object.values(params).some(Boolean)?'?'+new URLSearchParams(Object.entries(params).filter(([,v])=>v)):'');
const nav=path=>`<nav class="nav" aria-label="主导航">${[['/','Start'],['/community','Community'],['/me','Me']].map(([p,t])=>`<a href="${link(p)}" ${path===p||p!=='/'&&path.startsWith(p)?'aria-current="page"':''}>${t}</a>`).join('')}</nav>`;
const top=()=>'<div class="public-top"><a href="#/">‹ 首页</a><button type="button" data-refresh>刷新内容</button></div>';
const empty=(text)=>`<p class="empty">${esc(text)}</p>`;
const image=(r,cls='place-image')=>`<span class="${cls}">${r.mediaIds?.[0]?`<img src="/api/media/${esc(r.mediaIds[0])}" alt="" loading="lazy" data-photo>`:'照片待补'}</span>`;
const names=ids=>(ids||[]).map(id=>catalog.artists.find(a=>a.id===id)?.name).filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
const target=(kind,id)=>link('/'+kind+'/'+id);
const row=(kind,r,meta='',n=0)=>`<a class="review-place-row" href="${target(kind,r.id)}">${image(r,'place-image tone-'+n%4)}<span><strong>${esc(r.name)}</strong><small>${esc(meta||[r.city,r.category].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small></span><span aria-hidden="true">›</span></a>`;
const rows=(kind,items,text)=>items.length?items.map((r,n)=>row(kind,r,kind==='route'?`${r.city} · ${r.placeIds.length}站`:kind==='event'?`${r.city} · ${eventDate(r)}`:'',n)).join(''):empty(text);
const eventDate=r=>new Intl.DateTimeFormat('zh-CN',{timeZone:r.timeZone||'Asia/Shanghai',month:'numeric',day:'numeric',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}).format(new Date(r.startsAt));
function search(s){return `<form class="public-search" data-search><input type="search" name="q" aria-label="搜索艺人、城市、地点" maxlength="120" placeholder="艺人 / 城市 / 地点" value="${esc(s.q)}"><button type="submit">搜索</button></form>`;}
function filters(s,artist){const cityItems=[...new Set([...catalog.places.filter(p=>!artist||p.artistIds.includes(artist)),...catalog.routes.filter(r=>!artist||r.artistId===artist),...catalog.events.filter(r=>!artist||r.artistIds.includes(artist))].map(r=>r.city))].sort();return `<form class="filter-bar" data-filters>${!artist?`<label>艺人<select name="artistId"><option value="">全部艺人</option>${catalog.artists.map(a=>`<option value="${a.id}" ${s.artistId===a.id?'selected':''}>${esc(a.name)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`:''}<label>城市<select name="city"><option value="">全部城市</option>${[...new Set([...cityItems,...(s.city?[s.city]:[])])].map(c=>`<option ${s.city===c?'selected':''}>${esc(c)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label></form><nav class="category-rail" aria-label="地点分类">${['',...categories].map(c=>`<a href="${link(s.path,{q:s.q,artistId:s.artistId,city:s.city,category:c})}" ${s.category===c?'aria-current="true"':''}>${esc(c||'全部')}</a>`).join('')}</nav>`;}
const section=(title,body)=>`<section class="public-section"><h2>${title}</h2>${body}</section>`;
function discover(s,d,artist){return `${top()}<h1 class="view-title">${esc(artist?.name||'发现')}</h1>${search(s)}${filters(s,artist?.id)}${!artist?section('艺人',rows('artist',d.artists,'暂无匹配艺人')):''}${section('路线',rows('route',d.routes,'暂无已发布路线'))}${section('地点',rows('place',d.places,'暂无已发布地点'))}${section('演出',rows('event',d.events,'暂无已发布演出'))}`;}
function home(s,d){const records=[...d.routes.map(r=>({...r,kind:'route'})),...d.places.map(r=>({...r,kind:'place'})),...d.events.filter(e=>['upcoming','ongoing'].includes(e.status)).map(r=>({...r,kind:'event'}))].slice(0,12);return `<div class="public-top"><a href="${link('/discover',{city:s.city})}">${esc(s.city||'选择城市')} ⌄</a><button data-refresh>刷新内容</button></div><h1 class="home-brand" aria-label="START WITH…?"><span class="approved-title" aria-hidden="true"><img src="${art}" alt="" width="853" height="1844"></span></h1><div class="start-prompt"><label class="search-label" for="home-search">想从什么开始</label>${search(s)}</div>${filters(s)}<section class="recommend" aria-label="城市精选推荐"><div class="section-head"><h2>今天，从这里开始</h2>${records.length>1?'<button id="play" aria-label="暂停自动播放">暂停</button>':''}</div>${records.length?`<div class="viewport" tabindex="0" aria-label="精选卡片，可使用左右方向键切换"><div class="track">${records.map(r=>`<article class="recommend-card public-card ${r.kind==='place'?'place-slide':r.kind==='event'?'live-slide':''}"><div class="cover">${r.mediaIds?.[0]?`<img src="/api/media/${r.mediaIds[0]}" alt="" data-photo>`:'<div class="cover-empty">照片待补</div>'}<span class="cover-tag">${({route:'路线',place:'地点',event:'演出'})[r.kind]}</span></div><div class="card-body"><small class="card-kicker">${esc(r.city)} · ${esc(r.category||names(r.artistIds||[r.artistId]))}</small><h3>${esc(r.name)}</h3><a class="card-action" href="${target(r.kind,r.id)}">查看详情 <span>↗</span></a></div></article>`).join('')}</div></div>${records.length>1?'<div class="controls"><button id="prev" aria-label="上一张">‹</button><span id="count"></span><button id="next" aria-label="下一张">›</button></div>':''}`:empty('暂无已发布推荐')}</section><section class="public-section"><div class="section-head"><h2>这座城市值得去</h2><a href="${link('/discover',{city:s.city,artistId:s.artistId,category:s.category})}">全部地点 ↗</a></div>${rows('place',d.places,'暂无已发布地点')}</section>`;}
function market(s,d,community){const listings=d.listings.filter(l=>!community||l.communityId===community.id);return `<section class="public-market"><span class="public-badge">COMMUNITY</span><h2>物料集市</h2><p>塞我点物料吧</p>${listings.length?listings.map(r=>{const event=catalog.events.find(e=>e.id===r.eventId),p=catalog.places.find(p=>p.id===r.placeId);return `<article class="evidence">${image(r)}<h3><a href="#/listing/${r.id}">${esc(r.name)}</a></h3><p>${esc(r.description)}</p><span class="public-badge">${esc(names([r.artistId]))}</span>${event?`<div><a class="inline-link" href="${target('event',event.id)}">${esc(event.name)}</a></div>`:''}${p?`<a class="inline-link" href="${target('place',p.id)}">查看演出场馆</a>`:''}</article>`;}).join(''):empty('暂无可交换物料')}<div class="private-actions"><a href="#/market">发布与管理物料</a></div></section>`;}
function communityView(s,d,c){return `${top()}<h1>${esc(c?.name||'Community')}</h1>${c?.description?`<p>${esc(c.description)}</p>`:''}${c?.notice?section('公告',`<p>${esc(c.notice)}</p>`):''}${filters(s,c?.artist_id)}${!c?section('社群',d.communities.length?d.communities.map(c=>row('community',c,names([c.artist_id]))).join(''):empty('暂无已开放社群')):''}${section('路线',rows('route',d.routes,'暂无已发布路线'))}${section('地点',rows('place',d.places,'暂无已发布地点'))}${section('演出',rows('event',d.events,'暂无已发布演出'))}${market(s,d,c)}`;}
async function detail(kind,id,s){const r=await api.detail(kind,id);if(kind==='artist'){const d=await filtered({...s,artistId:id});return discover(s,d,r);}let html=`${top()}<header class="view-title"><p class="public-badge">${esc([r.city,r.category].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</p><h1>${esc(r.name)}</h1></header>`;if(r.mediaIds?.[0])html+=`<img class="hero-media" src="/api/media/${r.mediaIds[0]}" alt="${esc(r.name)}" data-photo>`;if(r.description)html+=`<p>${esc(r.description)}</p>`;
 if(kind==='place'){html+=`<a class="inline-link" href="#/book/new?placeId=${encodeURIComponent(id)}">记录打卡</a>`+section('打卡同款',(r.contributions||[]).map(c=>`<article class="evidence"><a class="inline-link" href="${target('artist',c.artistId)}">${esc(names([c.artistId])||'艺人资料已撤回')}</a><p>${esc(c.sameSpot)}</p><p class="public-badge">${esc(c.source)}</p>${(c.mediaIds||[]).map(id=>`<img class="hero-media" src="/api/media/${id}" alt="同款照片" data-photo>`).join('')}</article>`).join('')||empty('暂无已核对的艺人同款'))+section('地址',`<p>${esc(r.address||'地址待补')}</p>`)+section('相关路线',rows('route',r.routes,'暂无关联路线'))+section('相关演出',rows('event',catalog.events.filter(e=>e.placeId===id),'暂无关联演出'));}
 if(kind==='route'){html+=section('站点',r.placeIds.map((id,n)=>{const p=catalog.places.find(p=>p.id===id);return p?row('place',p,`${n+1} · ${p.category}`,n):`<p class="empty">${n+1} · 站点已撤回</p>`;}).join(''))+`<div class="related-links">${catalog.artists.some(a=>a.id===r.artistId)?`<a href="${target('artist',r.artistId)}">${esc(names([r.artistId]))}</a>`:''}</div>`;}
 if(kind==='event'){const p=catalog.places.find(p=>p.id===r.placeId);html+=section('演出信息',`<p>${esc(eventDate(r))} · ${esc(({upcoming:'未开始',ongoing:'进行中',ended:'已结束',cancelled:'已取消'})[r.status])}</p><div class="related-links">${r.artistIds.filter(id=>catalog.artists.some(a=>a.id===id)).map(id=>`<a href="${target('artist',id)}">${esc(names([id]))}</a>`).join('')}</div>`)+section('场馆',p?row('place',p):empty('场馆已撤回'))+market(s,{listings:catalog.listings.filter(l=>l.eventId===id)});}
 return html;
}
async function filtered(s){const params={};for(const k of ['city','artistId','category'])if(s[k])params[k]=s[k];const d=await api.public(params);if(!s.q.trim())return d;const q=s.q.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();for(const kind of ['artists','places','routes','events'])d[kind]=d[kind].filter(r=>[r.name,r.city,names(r.artistIds||[r.artistId])].some(v=>v?.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(q)));return d;}
async function privateData(){const sections=['memberships','notifications','submissions','exchanges','listings','book','doll'];try{const profile=await api.me('profile'),values=await Promise.all(sections.map(section=>api.me(section)));const result={profile,...Object.fromEntries(sections.map((section,index)=>[section,values[index]]))};result.memberships=result.memberships.map(item=>({...item,name:catalog.communities.find(c=>c.id===item.community_id)?.name||'社群'}));return result;}catch(error){return {authError:error.code||'AUTH_UNAVAILABLE'};}}
async function submitPrivate(form){if(form.dataset.submitting==='true')return;form.dataset.submitting='true';const button=form.querySelector('[type="submit"]');button.disabled=true;try{await performPrivateSubmission(form);}finally{delete form.dataset.submitting;button.disabled=false;}}
async function performPrivateSubmission(form){const status=form.querySelector('[data-form-status]'),data=new FormData(form),kind=form.dataset.kind;const required=kind==='route'?['name','city','artistId','source']:['name','city','artistId','category','source'];const missing=required.find(field=>!String(data.get(field)||'').trim());if(missing){status.hidden=false;status.textContent='请先填写带 * 的字段。';status.focus();return;}
 if(kind==='route'&&!form.routeStops?.length){status.hidden=false;status.textContent='请先从地图添加至少一个站点。';status.focus();return;}
 const mediaIds=[];for(const file of data.getAll('media'))if(file instanceof File&&file.size){try{mediaIds.push((await api.uploadImage(file)).id);}catch(error){status.hidden=false;status.textContent=error.code==='AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED'?'身份服务尚未接入，照片未上传。':'照片上传失败，请检查格式和大小。';status.focus();return;}}
 const value=kind==='route'?{name:String(data.get('name')).trim(),city:String(data.get('city')).trim(),artistId:String(data.get('artistId')),stops:routeSubmissionStops(form,String(data.get('source')).trim()),source:String(data.get('source')).trim(),description:String(data.get('description')||'').trim(),...(mediaIds.length?{mediaIds}:{} )}:{name:String(data.get('name')).trim(),city:String(data.get('city')).trim(),artistId:String(data.get('artistId')),category:String(data.get('category')),source:String(data.get('source')).trim(),sameSpot:String(data.get('sameSpot')||'').trim(),description:String(data.get('description')||'').trim(),...(mediaIds.length?{mediaIds}:{} )};
 if(kind==='place'&&form.selectedPoi)Object.assign(value,form.selectedPoi);
 try{await api.command(kind==='route'?'routeSubmission.create':'submission.create',value);status.hidden=false;status.textContent='已提交审核，结果会在通知里显示。';status.focus();form.reset();}catch(error){status.hidden=false;status.textContent=error.code==='AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED'?'身份服务尚未接入，暂时不能提交。':error.code==='INVALID_INPUT'?'请检查名称、城市、艺人和来源。':'提交失败，请稍后重试。';status.focus();}}
function mountCarousel(){const viewport=root.querySelector('.viewport');if(!viewport||root.querySelectorAll('.recommend-card').length<2)return;const region=root.querySelector('.recommend'),reduce=matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)'),embla=window.EmblaCarousel(viewport,{loop:true,align:'start',duration:reduce.matches?0:25}),play=root.querySelector('#play');let timer,paused=reduce.matches,hover=false,focus=false;const total=embla.slideNodes().length;const observer=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{inView=entries[0].isIntersecting;schedule();},{threshold:.4});let inView=true;observer.observe(viewport);function schedule(){clearTimeout(timer);const active=!paused&&!hover&&!focus&&!document.hidden&&!reduce.matches&&inView;region.dataset.rotation=active?'playing':'paused';play.textContent=paused?'播放':'暂停';play.setAttribute('aria-label',paused?'播放自动推荐':'暂停自动播放');play.disabled=reduce.matches;if(active)timer=setTimeout(()=>embla.scrollNext(),7000);}function update(){embla.slideNodes().forEach((node,i)=>{node.inert=i!==embla.selectedScrollSnap();});root.querySelector('#count').textContent=`${embla.selectedScrollSnap()+1} / ${total}`;schedule();}function move(delta){paused=true;delta>0?embla.scrollNext():embla.scrollPrev();schedule();}root.querySelector('#prev').onclick=()=>move(-1);root.querySelector('#next').onclick=()=>move(1);play.onclick=()=>{paused=!paused;schedule();};viewport.onkeydown=e=>{if(['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight'].includes(e.key)){e.preventDefault();move(e.key==='ArrowRight'?1:-1);}};region.onmouseenter=()=>{hover=true;schedule();};region.onmouseleave=()=>{hover=false;schedule();};region.onfocusin=()=>{focus=true;schedule();};region.onfocusout=e=>{focus=region.contains(e.relatedTarget);schedule();};embla.on('select',update).on('pointerDown',()=>{paused=true;schedule();});const change=()=>{paused=reduce.matches;embla.reInit({duration:reduce.matches?0:25});schedule();};reduce.addEventListener('change',change);document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',schedule);update();cleanup=()=>{clearTimeout(timer);observer.disconnect();embla.destroy();reduce.removeEventListener('change',change);document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange',schedule);};}
async function render(){const current=++generation,s=state();window.START_PUBLIC_READY=false;cleanup();cleanup=()=>{};root.innerHTML=`<main class="page">${top()}<p class="loading" role="status">正在载入…</p></main>${nav(s.path)}`;
 try{const fetched=await api.public();if(current!==generation)return;catalog=fetched;let content,cls='';const match=/^\/(artist|place|route|event)\/([^/]+)$/.exec(s.path),communityId=/^\/community\/([^/]+)$/.exec(s.path);if(match)content=await detail(match[1],match[2],s);else if(s.path==='/book'||s.path.startsWith('/book/')||s.path.startsWith('/share/')){content=`<div class="public-top"><a href="#/book">‹ START Book</a><a href="#/me">Me</a></div><div class="submission-shell book-workspace" data-book-workspace><p role="status">正在载入…</p></div>`;}else if(s.path==='/market'||s.path.startsWith('/listing/')){content=`${top()}<div class="submission-shell" data-market-workspace><p role="status">正在载入…</p></div>`;}else if(s.path==='/me'){content=renderPrivateMe(await privateData());}else if(s.path==='/submit'){content=renderSubmissionForm({kind:s.kind,artists:catalog.artists});}else{const c=communityId?catalog.communities.find(c=>c.id===communityId[1]):null;if(communityId&&!c)throw Object.assign(Error('NOT_FOUND'),{status:404});const d=await filtered({...s,artistId:c?.artist_id||s.artistId});if(s.path==='/'){content=home(s,d);cls='home';}else if(s.path==='/discover')content=discover(s,d);else if(s.path==='/community'||c)content=communityView(s,d,c);else throw Object.assign(Error('NOT_FOUND'),{status:404});}if(current!==generation)return;root.innerHTML=`<main class="page ${cls}">${content}</main>${nav(s.path)}`;root.querySelectorAll('[data-photo]').forEach(img=>img.addEventListener('error',()=>{const note=document.createElement('span');note.className='public-badge';note.textContent='照片暂不可用';img.replaceWith(note);},{once:true}));if(cls==='home')mountCarousel();if(root.querySelector('[data-book-workspace]'))cleanup=mountBook(root,api,{path:s.path,placeId:new URLSearchParams(location.hash.split('?')[1]||'').get('placeId')||'',catalog});if(s.path==='/market'||s.path.startsWith('/listing/'))cleanup=mountMarket(root,catalog,api,s.path.startsWith('/listing/')?s.path.slice(9):null);if(s.path==='/community'||communityId)cleanup=mountCommunityMembership(root,catalog,api,communityId?.[1]);if(s.path==='/submit')cleanup=mountSubmissionTools(root.querySelector('[data-private-submit]'),catalog,api);document.title='START · '+(root.querySelector('h1')?.textContent?.trim()||'发现');window.START_PUBLIC_LOCATION=location.hash;window.START_PUBLIC_READY=true;}
 catch(e){if(current!==generation)return;root.innerHTML=`<main class="page">${top()}<div class="error-box"><h1>${e.status===404?'内容已撤回或不存在':'暂时无法载入内容'}</h1><button data-refresh>重试</button></div></main>${nav(s.path)}`;window.START_PUBLIC_LOCATION=location.hash;window.START_PUBLIC_READY=true;}}

FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/public-ui/route-stops.mjs
================================================================================
import Sortable from './sortable.mjs';
const categories=['美食','咖啡','娱乐','景点','书店','其他'];
export function mountRouteStops(form,catalog,status){
 const list=form.querySelector('[data-route-stops]');
 const stops=[];form.routeStops=stops;
 let keyboardSnapshot=null;
 const motion=matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
 const sorter=new Sortable(list,{handle:'.route-stop-handle',draggable:'[data-route-stop]',forceFallback:true,animation:motion.matches?0:180,ghostClass:'route-stop-ghost',chosenClass:'route-stop-chosen',fallbackOnBody:true,
  onEnd({oldDraggableIndex:from,newDraggableIndex:to}){
   if(Number.isInteger(from)&&Number.isInteger(to)&&from!==to)stops.splice(to,0,stops.splice(from,1)[0]);
   render(to);status(`已放到第 ${to+1} 站。`);
  }
 });
 const updateMotion=()=>sorter.option('animation',motion.matches?0:180);
 motion.addEventListener('change',updateMotion);
 function render(focusIndex){
  list.replaceChildren();
  if(!stops.length){const p=document.createElement('p');p.className='empty';p.textContent='从地图搜索添加站点，再按出行顺序排列。';list.append(p);return;}
  stops.forEach((stop,index)=>{
   const row=document.createElement('li');row.dataset.routeStop='';
   const handle=document.createElement('button');handle.type='button';handle.className='route-stop-handle';handle.textContent='⠿ 拖动排序';handle.setAttribute('aria-label',`拖动第 ${index+1} 站排序`);handle.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(Boolean(keyboardSnapshot)));handle.title='按空格拿起，方向键调整，空格放下，Esc 取消';
   handle.onkeydown=event=>{
    if([' ','Enter'].includes(event.key)){
     event.preventDefault();keyboardSnapshot=keyboardSnapshot?null:[...stops];sorter.option('disabled',Boolean(keyboardSnapshot));render(index);status(keyboardSnapshot?'已拿起。使用上下方向键调整，空格放下，Esc 取消。':'已确认站点顺序。');
    }else if(keyboardSnapshot&&['ArrowUp','ArrowDown'].includes(event.key)){
     event.preventDefault();const next=index+(event.key==='ArrowUp'?-1:1);if(next<0||next>=stops.length)return;
     stops.splice(next,0,stops.splice(index,1)[0]);render(next);status(`当前第 ${next+1} 站，共 ${stops.length} 站。`);
    }else if(keyboardSnapshot&&event.key==='Escape'){
     event.preventDefault();const original=keyboardSnapshot.indexOf(stops[index]);stops.splice(0,stops.length,...keyboardSnapshot);keyboardSnapshot=null;sorter.option('disabled',false);render(original);status('已取消排序。');
    }
   };
   row.append(handle);
   const place=stop.place||catalog.places.find(p=>p.id===stop.placeId);
   const heading=document.createElement('strong');heading.textContent=place?.name||'地点已撤回';
   const meta=document.createElement('small');meta.textContent=`第 ${index+1} 站 · ${place?.city||''} · ${stop.place?'新地点，随路线审核':'已收录地点'}`;
   row.append(heading,meta);
   if(stop.place){
    for(const [key,label] of [['category','类别'],['sameSpot','打卡同款'],['source','站点来源']]){
     const wrapper=document.createElement('label');wrapper.textContent=label;
     const input=document.createElement(key==='category'?'select':'input');input.setAttribute('aria-label',`第 ${index+1} 站${label}`);
     if(key==='category'){for(const category of categories){const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=category;option.textContent=category;input.append(option);}}
     else {input.maxLength=3500;input.placeholder=key==='source'?'留空沿用路线来源':'机位、作品或照片说明';}
     input.value=stop.place[key]||'';input.addEventListener('input',()=>{stop.place[key]=input.value;});wrapper.append(input);row.append(wrapper);
    }
   }
   const controls=document.createElement('div');controls.className='route-stop-controls';
   const remove=document.createElement('button');remove.type='button';remove.className='secondary';remove.textContent='移除';remove.setAttribute('aria-label',`移除第 ${index+1} 站`);
   remove.onclick=()=>{keyboardSnapshot=null;sorter.option('disabled',false);stops.splice(index,1);render(Math.min(index,stops.length-1));status('已移除站点。');};
   controls.append(remove);
   row.append(controls);list.append(row);
  });
  if(focusIndex!==undefined)list.querySelectorAll('[data-route-stop]')[focusIndex]?.querySelector('button:not(:disabled)')?.focus();
 }
 const reset=()=>{stops.length=0;keyboardSnapshot=null;sorter.option('disabled',false);render();};
 render();form.addEventListener('reset',reset);
 const add=poi=>{
  keyboardSnapshot=null;sorter.option('disabled',false);
  const existing=catalog.places.find(p=>p.poiId===poi.providerPoiId);
  if(stops.some(s=>(existing&&s.placeId===existing.id)||s.place?.poiId===poi.providerPoiId)){status('该站点已在路线中。');return;}
  if(stops.length>=30){status('一条投稿路线最多添加 30 个站点。');return;}
  stops.push(existing?{placeId:existing.id}:{place:{name:poi.name,city:poi.city,category:'其他',poiId:poi.providerPoiId,address:String(poi.address||'').slice(0,500),longitude:poi.longitude,latitude:poi.latitude,coordinateSystem:'gcj02',source:'',sameSpot:''}});
  render();status(`已加入第 ${stops.length} 站：${poi.name}`);
 };
 add.destroy=()=>{sorter.destroy();motion.removeEventListener('change',updateMotion);form.removeEventListener('reset',reset);};
 return add;
}

export function routeSubmissionStops(form,source){
 return (form.routeStops||[]).map(stop=>stop.place?{place:{...stop.place,source:stop.place.source?.trim()||source}}:{placeId:stop.placeId});
}

FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/public-ui/submission-tools.mjs

// Candidate extraction never publishes content or supplies invented coordinates.
import {createPlatformClient} from '/client.mjs';
import {mountRouteStops} from './route-stops.mjs';
export function mountSubmissionTools(form, catalog, api=createPlatformClient()) {
  const lifecycle = new AbortController();
  const signal = lifecycle.signal;
  let dialog;
  const field = name => form.elements.namedItem(name);
  const status = text => { const node=form.querySelector('[data-form-status]'); node.hidden=false; node.textContent=text; };
  const addRouteStop=form.dataset.kind==='route'?mountRouteStops(form,catalog,status):null;
  const close = () => { if(dialog){dialog.close();dialog.remove();dialog=null;} };
  const validPoi = p => p?.provider==='amap' && typeof p.providerPoiId==='string' && p.providerPoiId.length>0 && p.providerPoiId.length<=100 && typeof p.name==='string' && p.name.length<=200 && typeof p.city==='string' && p.city.length<=200 && Number.isFinite(p.longitude) && Math.abs(p.longitude)<=180 && Number.isFinite(p.latitude) && Math.abs(p.latitude)<=90 && p.coordinateSystem==='gcj02';
  function choose(p) {
    if(form.dataset.kind==='route') {
      addRouteStop(p);return;
    }
    field('name').value=p.name;field('city').value=p.city;
    form.selectedPoi={poiId:p.providerPoiId,address:String(p.address||'').slice(0,500),longitude:p.longitude,latitude:p.latitude,coordinateSystem:'gcj02'};
    status(`已选中：${p.name} · ${p.address||p.city}。请核对艺人、同款和来源后提交。`);
  }
  function openMap(keyword,city) {
    close();const token=crypto.randomUUID();
    dialog=document.createElement('dialog');dialog.className='submission-map-dialog';
    const button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';button.textContent='关闭地图';button.className='secondary';button.onclick=close;
    const frame=document.createElement('iframe');frame.title='高德地图选点';
    frame.src='/admin/map-picker.html?'+new URLSearchParams({city:city||field('city').value,keyword:keyword||field('mapQuery').value,parent:location.origin,token});
    const currentDialog=dialog;
    const receive = event => {
      if(event.origin!==location.origin||event.source!==frame.contentWindow||event.data?.token!==token)return;
      if(event.data.type==='start-map-close')close();
      if(event.data.type==='start-map-selection'&&validPoi(event.data.poi)){choose(event.data.poi);close();}
    };
    window.addEventListener('message',receive,{signal});
    currentDialog.addEventListener('close',()=>{window.removeEventListener('message',receive);currentDialog.remove();if(dialog===currentDialog)dialog=null;},{once:true});
    dialog.append(button,frame);document.body.append(dialog);dialog.showModal();button.focus();
  }
  form.querySelector('[data-map-search]').addEventListener('click',()=>openMap(),{signal});
  // Editing the location invalidates the previous map binding, preventing stale coordinates.
  for(const name of ['name','city'])field(name).addEventListener('input',()=>{delete form.selectedPoi;},{signal});
  form.addEventListener('reset',()=>{delete form.selectedPoi;form.querySelector('[data-extract-results]')?.remove();},{signal});
  form.querySelector('[data-text-import]').addEventListener('click',async event=>{
    const text=field('textImport').value.trim(),button=event.currentTarget;
    if(!text){status('请先粘贴需要识别的文字。');return;}
    if(text.length>3500){status('每次最多识别 3500 字，请分段粘贴。');return;}
    button.disabled=true;status('正在本机识别地点，请稍候…');form.querySelector('[data-extract-results]')?.remove();
    try {
      const data=await api.extract({text,city:field('city').value},AbortSignal.any([signal,AbortSignal.timeout(95000)]));
      if(!Array.isArray(data.items))throw Error('INVALID_RESPONSE');
      const list=document.createElement('div');list.dataset.extractResults='';list.className='extraction-results';
      for(const item of data.items){
        if(typeof item.name!=='string'||!text.includes(item.name))continue;
        const row=document.createElement('div'),label=document.createElement('p'),use=document.createElement('button');
        label.textContent=[item.name,item.city].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');use.type='button';use.className='secondary';use.textContent='核对地图位置';
        use.addEventListener('click',()=>{field('mapQuery').value=item.name;openMap(item.name,item.city);},{signal});row.append(label,use);if(item.warning){const warning=document.createElement('p');warning.textContent=item.warning;row.append(warning);}list.append(row);
      }
      button.after(list);status(list.childElementCount?`识别到 ${list.childElementCount} 个候选地点，请逐个核对地图位置；不会自动提交。`:'未识别到可靠地点，请补充城市和具体名称，或直接使用地图搜索。');
    } catch(error) { if(!signal.aborted)status(['AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED','UNAUTHENTICATED'].includes(error.code)?'文字识别需要登录身份；当前身份服务尚未接入。原文已保留，可先使用地图搜索。':'识别暂不可用或超时，原文已保留。可以重试或使用地图搜索。'); }
    finally {button.disabled=false;}
  },{signal});
  return () => {lifecycle.abort();close();addRouteStop?.destroy();};
}

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';import {execFileSync} from 'node:child_process';import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import {Pool} from './src/runtime.mjs';
import {createPlatform} from './src/platform.mjs';
import {createMedia} from './src/media.mjs';
import {createServer} from './src/http.mjs';
import {serveStatic} from './src/static.mjs';
import {createIntegrations} from './src/integrations.mjs';
import {createProfileRepository} from '../START_ACCOUNT_FOUNDATION_2026-10-05/src/profile-repository.mjs';
const pool=new Pool({host:'127.0.0.1',port:55435,user:'start_local',database:'start_acceptance',max:8});
await pool.query(await fs.readFile(new URL('../START_ACCOUNT_FOUNDATION_2026-10-05/db/001_accounts.sql',import.meta.url),'utf8'));

async function shutdown(){server.closeAllConnections();server.close(async()=>{await pool.end();process.exit(0);});}
process.on('SIGINT',shutdown);process.on('SIGTERM',shutdown);

FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/src/community.mjs

import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {fail} from './contracts.mjs';
export async function communityCommand(ctx,op,v){
 const {client,userId,one,admin,content,notify}=ctx;
 const manager=async(id)=>{const row=await one("SELECT * FROM start_app.communities WHERE id=$1 AND status='active'",[id]);if(row.manager_id!==userId)await admin();return row;};
 if(op==='community.apply'){
  await content(v.artistId,'artist');if((await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM start_app.communities WHERE artist_id=$1',[v.artistId])).rowCount)fail('CONFLICT',409);
  const pending=(await client.query("SELECT id FROM start_app.community_applications WHERE artist_id=$1 AND status='pending'",[v.artistId])).rows[0];if(pending)fail('CONFLICT',409);
  const id=randomUUID();await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.community_applications(id,user_id,artist_id,data) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4)',[id,userId,v.artistId,JSON.stringify(v)]);return {id};
 }
 if(op==='community.review'){
  await admin();const a=await one('SELECT * FROM start_app.community_applications WHERE id=$1',[v.id]);if(a.status==='approved'&&v.decision==='approve')return {id:a.community_id};if(a.status!=='pending')fail('CONFLICT',409);
  if(v.decision==='reject'){if(!v.reason)fail('INVALID_INPUT');await client.query("UPDATE start_app.community_applications SET status='rejected',reason=$2 WHERE id=$1",[a.id,v.reason]);await notify(a.user_id,'community.rejected',a.id);return {id:a.id};}
  await content(a.artist_id,'artist');await one("SELECT id FROM start_accounts.users WHERE id=$1 AND status='active'",[a.user_id]);const id=randomUUID();await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.communities(id,artist_id,manager_id,name,description) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,$5)',[id,a.artist_id,a.user_id,a.data.name,a.data.description]);
  await client.query("INSERT INTO start_app.memberships(community_id,user_id,status) VALUES($1,$2,'joined')",[id,a.user_id]);
  await client.query("UPDATE start_app.community_applications SET status='approved',community_id=$2 WHERE id=$1",[a.id,id]);await notify(a.user_id,'community.approved',id);return {id};
 }
 if(op==='community.join'){await one("SELECT id FROM start_app.communities WHERE id=$1 AND status='active'",[v.id]);await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.memberships(community_id,user_id,status) VALUES($1,$2,$3) ON CONFLICT(community_id,user_id) DO UPDATE SET status=excluded.status',[v.id,userId,v.status]);return {id:v.id,status:v.status};}
 if(op==='community.edit'){await manager(v.id);await client.query('UPDATE start_app.communities SET description=COALESCE($2,description),notice=COALESCE($3,notice) WHERE id=$1',[v.id,v.description??null,v.notice??null]);return {id:v.id};}
 if(op==='community.member'){const c=await manager(v.id);if(c.manager_id===v.userId)fail('CONFLICT',409);await one("SELECT id FROM start_accounts.users WHERE id=$1 AND status='active'",[v.userId]);await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.memberships(community_id,user_id,status) VALUES($1,$2,$3) ON CONFLICT(community_id,user_id) DO UPDATE SET status=excluded.status',[v.id,v.userId,v.status]);return {id:v.id};}
 if(op==='community.request'){const c=await manager(v.id);if(v.kind==='transfer'){if(!v.targetUserId)fail('INVALID_INPUT');await one("SELECT id FROM start_accounts.users WHERE id=$1 AND status='active'",[v.targetUserId]);}const id=randomUUID();await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.community_requests(id,community_id,user_id,data) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4)',[id,v.id,userId,JSON.stringify({...v,managerId:c.manager_id})]);return {id};}
 if(op==='community.request.review'){
  await admin();const r=await one("SELECT * FROM start_app.community_requests WHERE id=$1 AND status='pending'",[v.id]);if(v.decision==='reject'&&!v.reason)fail('INVALID_INPUT');
  if(v.decision==='approve'){const c=await one('SELECT manager_id,status FROM start_app.communities WHERE id=$1',[r.community_id]);if(c.status!=='active'||c.manager_id!==r.data.managerId)fail('CONFLICT',409);await one("SELECT id FROM start_accounts.users WHERE id=$1 AND status='active'",[r.user_id]);}
  if(v.decision==='approve'&&r.data.kind==='transfer'){await one("SELECT id FROM start_accounts.users WHERE id=$1 AND status='active'",[r.data.targetUserId]);await client.query('UPDATE start_app.communities SET manager_id=$2 WHERE id=$1',[r.community_id,r.data.targetUserId]);await client.query("INSERT INTO start_app.memberships(community_id,user_id,status) VALUES($1,$2,'joined') ON CONFLICT(community_id,user_id) DO UPDATE SET status='joined'",[r.community_id,r.data.targetUserId]);}
  if(v.decision==='approve'&&r.data.kind==='dissolve')await client.query("UPDATE start_app.communities SET status='dissolved' WHERE id=$1",[r.community_id]);
  if(v.decision==='approve'&&r.data.kind==='change')fail('INVALID_INPUT'); // Content changes require explicit editor fields, not arbitrary prose.
  await client.query('UPDATE start_app.community_requests SET status=$2 WHERE id=$1',[r.id,v.decision==='approve'?'approved':'rejected']);await notify(r.user_id,'community.request.reviewed',r.id);return {id:r.id};
 }
 return null;
}
