import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {fail,parse} from './contracts.mjs';
export async function saveContent(ctx,kind,v){
 const {client,content,media}=ctx;
 await media(v.mediaIds);
 if(kind==='place'&&v.artistEvidence)await content(v.artistEvidence.artistId,'artist');
 if(kind==='route'){await content(v.artistId,'artist');for(const id of v.placeIds)await content(id,'place',false);}
 if(kind==='event'){for(const id of v.artistIds)await content(id,'artist');await content(v.placeId,'place',false);}
 const old=v.id?await content(v.id,kind,false):null;
 if(old&&v.version!==old.version)fail('CONFLICT',409);
 const id=old?.id||randomUUID(),version=(old?.version||0)+1;
 const {id:ignore,version:ignored,...data}=v;
 await client.query(`INSERT INTO start_app.content(id,kind,version,draft,poi_id) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,$5) ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET version=excluded.version,draft=excluded.draft,poi_id=excluded.poi_id,updated_at=now()`,[id,kind,version,JSON.stringify(data),kind==='place'?(v.poiId??old?.poi_id??null):null]);
 await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.revisions(content_id,version,data) VALUES($1,$2,$3)',[id,version,JSON.stringify(data)]);
 return {id,version,...data};
}
export async function contentCommand(ctx,op,v){
 const {client,content,admin,owns,notify,userId,one}=ctx;
 if(op==='import.places'){await admin();const ids=[];for(const item of v.items){const old=item.poiId?(await client.query('SELECT id FROM start_app.content WHERE poi_id=$1',[item.poiId])).rows[0]:null;ids.push(old?.id||(await saveContent(ctx,'place',item)).id);}return {ids};}
 if(op==='home.configure'){await admin();for(const id of v.ids)await one('SELECT id FROM start_app.content WHERE id=$1 AND published IS NOT NULL',[id]);await client.query("INSERT INTO start_app.settings(key,data) VALUES('home',$1) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET data=excluded.data",[JSON.stringify(v)]);return {ok:true};}
 if(op==='correction.create'){await content(v.id,v.kind);parse(v.kind+'.save',{...v.data,id:v.id,version:v.version});const id=randomUUID();await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.corrections(id,user_id,content_id,data) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4)',[id,userId,v.id,JSON.stringify(v)]);return {id};}
 if(op==='correction.review'){await admin();const r=await one('SELECT * FROM start_app.corrections WHERE id=$1',[v.id]);if(r.status==='approved'&&v.decision==='approve')return {id:r.content_id};if(r.status!=='pending')fail('CONFLICT',409);if(v.decision==='reject'){if(!v.reason)fail('INVALID_INPUT');await client.query("UPDATE start_app.corrections SET status='rejected',reason=$2 WHERE id=$1",[r.id,v.reason]);}else{if(!v.checked)fail('INVALID_INPUT');const p=parse(r.data.kind+'.save',{...r.data.data,id:r.content_id,version:r.data.version});await saveContent(ctx,r.data.kind,p);await contentCommand(ctx,'content.publish',{id:r.content_id,version:p.version+1});await client.query("UPDATE start_app.corrections SET status='approved' WHERE id=$1",[r.id]);}await notify(r.user_id,'correction.reviewed',r.id);return {id:r.content_id};}
 if(op.endsWith('.save')){await admin();return saveContent(ctx,op.split('.')[0],v);}
 if(op==='content.publish'){
  await admin();const r=await one('SELECT * FROM start_app.content WHERE id=$1',[v.id]);if(r.version!==v.version)fail('CONFLICT',409);
  if(r.kind==='place'&&r.draft.artistEvidence)await content(r.draft.artistEvidence.artistId,'artist');
  if(r.kind==='route'){await content(r.draft.artistId,'artist');for(const id of r.draft.placeIds)await content(id,'place');}
  if(r.kind==='event'){for(const id of r.draft.artistIds)await content(id,'artist');await content(r.draft.placeId,'place');}
  if(r.draft.mediaIds?.length)await client.query("UPDATE start_app.media SET visibility='public' WHERE id=ANY($1::uuid[])",[r.draft.mediaIds]);
  await client.query('UPDATE start_app.content SET published=draft WHERE id=$1',[r.id]);return {id:r.id,version:r.version};
 }
 if(op==='content.withdraw'){await admin();await one('UPDATE start_app.content SET published=NULL WHERE id=$1 RETURNING id',[v.id]);return {id:v.id};}
 if(op==='content.restore'){await admin();const r=await one('SELECT kind,version FROM start_app.content WHERE id=$1',[v.id]);if(r.version!==v.version)fail('CONFLICT',409);const previous=await one('SELECT data FROM start_app.revisions WHERE content_id=$1 AND version=$2',[v.id,v.fromVersion]);return saveContent(ctx,r.kind,{...previous.data,id:v.id,version:r.version});}
 if(op==='submission.create'||op==='routeSubmission.create'){
  await content(v.artistId,'artist');await ctx.media(v.mediaIds);const id=randomUUID();
  if(op==='routeSubmission.create')for(const stop of v.stops||v.placeIds.map(placeId=>({placeId}))){if(stop.placeId)await content(stop.placeId,'place');else await ctx.media(stop.place.mediaIds);}
  await client.query("INSERT INTO start_app.submissions(id,user_id,artist_id,data,status,kind) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,'pending',$5)",[id,userId,v.artistId,JSON.stringify(v),op==='routeSubmission.create'?'route':'place']);return {id,status:'pending'};
 }
 if(op==='submission.resubmit'){
  const s=owns(await one('SELECT * FROM start_app.submissions WHERE id=$1',[v.id]));if(s.status!=='rejected')fail('CONFLICT',409);
  await client.query("UPDATE start_app.submissions SET data=data||$2::jsonb,status='pending',reason=NULL WHERE id=$1",[s.id,JSON.stringify({source:v.source})]);return {id:s.id,status:'pending'};
 }
 if(op==='submission.review'){
  await admin();const s=await one('SELECT * FROM start_app.submissions WHERE id=$1',[v.id]);
  if(s.status==='approved'&&v.decision==='approve')return {id:s.content_id||s.place_id};if(s.status!=='pending')fail('CONFLICT',409);
  if(v.decision==='reject'){if(!v.reason)fail('INVALID_INPUT');await client.query("UPDATE start_app.submissions SET status='rejected',reason=$2 WHERE id=$1",[s.id,v.reason]);await notify(s.user_id,'submission.rejected',s.id);return {id:s.id,status:'rejected'};}
  if(v.checked!==true)fail('INVALID_INPUT');await content(s.artist_id,'artist');
  await one("SELECT id FROM start_accounts.users WHERE id=$1 AND status='active'",[s.user_id]);await ctx.media(s.data.mediaIds,s.user_id);
  if(s.kind==='route'){
   const placeIds=[];
   for(const stop of s.data.stops||s.data.placeIds.map(placeId=>({placeId}))){
    if(stop.placeId){await content(stop.placeId,'place');placeIds.push(stop.placeId);continue;}
    // A child contribution uses the same review transaction. A later failure rolls everything back.
    const childId=randomUUID(),data={...stop.place,artistId:s.artist_id};
    await client.query("INSERT INTO start_app.submissions(id,user_id,artist_id,data,status,kind) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,'pending','place')",[childId,s.user_id,s.artist_id,JSON.stringify(data)]);
    const added=await contentCommand(ctx,'submission.review',{id:childId,decision:'approve',checked:true});placeIds.push(added.id);
   }
   if(new Set(placeIds).size!==placeIds.length)fail('INVALID_INPUT');
   const {stops,...data}=s.data;
   const created=await saveContent({...ctx,media:ids=>ctx.media(ids,s.user_id)},'route',{...data,placeIds});
   await contentCommand(ctx,'content.publish',{id:created.id,version:created.version});await client.query("UPDATE start_app.submissions SET status='approved',content_id=$2 WHERE id=$1",[s.id,created.id]);await notify(s.user_id,'submission.approved',s.id);return {id:created.id};
  }
  const existing=s.data.poiId?(await client.query('SELECT * FROM start_app.content WHERE poi_id=$1',[s.data.poiId])).rows[0]:null;
  if(existing&&!existing.published)fail('CONFLICT',409);
  let p=existing;
  if(!p){const {artistId,sameSpot,...data}=s.data;const created=await saveContent({...ctx,media:ids=>ctx.media(ids,s.user_id)},'place',data);await client.query('UPDATE start_app.content SET published=draft WHERE id=$1',[created.id]);p={id:created.id};}
  await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.contributions(id,submission_id,place_id,artist_id,data) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,$5)',[randomUUID(),s.id,p.id,s.artist_id,JSON.stringify({source:s.data.source,sameSpot:s.data.sameSpot||'',mediaIds:s.data.mediaIds||[]})]);
  if(s.data.mediaIds?.length)await client.query("UPDATE start_app.media SET visibility='public' WHERE id=ANY($1::uuid[])",[s.data.mediaIds]);
  await client.query("UPDATE start_app.submissions SET status='approved',place_id=$2,content_id=$2 WHERE id=$1",[s.id,p.id]);await notify(s.user_id,'submission.approved',s.id);return {id:p.id};
 }
 return null;
}

import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {fail} from './contracts.mjs';
import {publiclyReferenced} from './public-media.mjs';
export function context(client,userId){
 const one=async(sql,values=[])=>{const r=await client.query(sql,values);if(!r.rows[0])fail('NOT_FOUND',404);return r.rows[0];};
 const content=async(id,kind,published=true)=>{const r=await one('SELECT * FROM start_app.content WHERE id=$1 AND kind=$2',[id,kind]);if(published&&!r.published)fail('NOT_FOUND',404);return r;};
 const admin=async()=>{if(!(await client.query('SELECT 1 FROM start_app.admins WHERE user_id=$1',[userId])).rowCount)fail('FORBIDDEN',403);};
 const owns=row=>{if(row.user_id!==userId)fail('FORBIDDEN',403);return row;};
 const notify=async(owner,kind,id)=>client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.notifications(id,user_id,kind,reference_id) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4)',[randomUUID(),owner,kind,id]);
 const media=async(ids=[],owner=userId)=>{for(const id of ids){const row=await one('SELECT * FROM start_app.media WHERE id=$1',[id]);if(row.user_id!==owner&&(row.visibility!=='public'||!await publiclyReferenced(client,id)))fail('FORBIDDEN',403);}};
 return {client,userId,one,content,admin,owns,notify,media};
}

export class PlatformError extends Error {constructor(code,status=400){super(code);this.code=code;this.status=status;}}
export function fail(code,status=400){throw new PlatformError(code,status);}
export function parse(op,value){const r=schemas[op]?.safeParse(value);if(!r?.success)fail('INVALID_INPUT');return r.data;}

import http from 'node:http';
import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {PlatformError,fail} from './contracts.mjs';
import {validateIdentity} from '../../START_ACCOUNT_FOUNDATION_2026-10-05/src/accounts.mjs';
const json=(res,status,value)=>{res.writeHead(status,{'content-type':'application/json; charset=utf-8','cache-control':'no-store','x-content-type-options':'nosniff','referrer-policy':'no-referrer'});res.end(JSON.stringify(value));};
async function readBody(req,limit){let size=0;const chunks=[];for await(const b of req){size+=b.length;if(size>limit)fail('TOO_LARGE',413);chunks.push(b);}return Buffer.concat(chunks);}
export function createServer({app,media,repository,verifyBearer,allowedOrigins=[],serveStatic,serveIntegrations}){
 async function actor(req){if(typeof verifyBearer!=='function')fail('AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED',503);const a=req.headers.authorization;if(typeof a!=='string'||a.length>8192||!/^Bearer [^\s]+$/i.test(a))fail('UNAUTHENTICATED',401);let identity;try{identity=await verifyBearer(a.slice(7));}catch{fail('AUTH_UNAVAILABLE',503);}return {id:await repository.resolveIdentity(validateIdentity(identity))};}
 const server=http.createServer(async(req,res)=>{
  const requestId=randomUUID();res.setHeader('x-request-id',requestId);res.setHeader('referrer-policy','no-referrer');
  try{
   if(!/^(127\.0\.0\.1|localhost)(:\d+)?$/.test(req.headers.host||''))fail('FORBIDDEN',403);
   const url=new URL(req.url,'http://'+req.headers.host);
   const origin=req.headers.origin;
   if(origin&&origin!==url.origin&&!allowedOrigins.includes(origin))fail('FORBIDDEN',403);
   if(req.headers['sec-fetch-site']==='cross-site'&&!allowedOrigins.includes(origin))fail('FORBIDDEN',403);
   if(origin&&allowedOrigins.includes(origin)){res.setHeader('access-control-allow-origin',origin);res.setHeader('vary','Origin');}
   if(req.method==='OPTIONS'){if(!origin||!allowedOrigins.includes(origin))fail('FORBIDDEN',403);res.writeHead(204,{'access-control-allow-methods':'GET,POST','access-control-allow-headers':'authorization,content-type,idempotency-key'});res.end();return;}
   if(req.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/api/status'){json(res,200,{mode:'local',database:true,authConfigured:typeof verifyBearer==='function',cloudConnected:false});return;}
   if(req.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/api/home'){json(res,200,await app.home());return;}
   if(req.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/api/public'){const filters={};for(const [k,v] of url.searchParams){if(Object.hasOwn(filters,k))fail('INVALID_INPUT');filters[k]=v;}json(res,200,await app.publicQuery(filters));return;}
   const detail=/^\/api\/public\/(artist|place|route|event|listing)\/([^/]+)$/.exec(url.pathname);if(req.method==='GET'&&detail){json(res,200,await app.publicDetail(detail[1],detail[2]));return;}
   const adminPath=/^\/api\/admin\/(content|submissions|applications|requests|audit|revisions|media)$/.exec(url.pathname);if(req.method==='GET'&&adminPath){json(res,200,await app.adminQuery(await actor(req),adminPath[1],url.searchParams.get('id')));return;}
   const sharedImage=/^\/api\/share\/([^/]+)\/media\/([^/]+)$/.exec(url.pathname);if(req.method==='GET'&&sharedImage){const owner=await app.shareMediaOwner(sharedImage[1],sharedImage[2]),m=await media.read(owner,sharedImage[2]);res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':m.mime,'cache-control':'no-store','x-content-type-options':'nosniff'});res.end(m.bytes);return;}
   if(req.method==='GET'&&url.pathname.startsWith('/api/share/')){json(res,200,await app.readShare(url.pathname.slice(11)));return;}
   if(req.method==='GET'&&url.pathname.startsWith('/api/me/')){json(res,200,await app.privateQuery(await actor(req),url.pathname.slice(8)));return;}
   if(req.method==='GET'&&url.pathname==='/api/recommend'){if([...url.searchParams.keys()].some(k=>k!=='city'))fail('INVALID_INPUT');json(res,200,await app.recommend(req.headers.authorization?await actor(req):null,{city:url.searchParams.get('city')}));return;}
   if(req.method==='POST'&&url.pathname==='/api/media'){const a=await actor(req);json(res,201,await media.upload(a,await readBody(req,5*1024*1024),req.headers['content-type']));return;}
   if(req.method==='GET'&&url.pathname.startsWith('/api/media/')){const a=req.headers.authorization?await actor(req):null;const m=await media.read(a,decodeURIComponent(url.pathname.slice(11)));res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':m.mime,'cache-control':'no-store','x-content-type-options':'nosniff'});res.end(m.bytes);return;}
   if(req.method==='POST'&&url.pathname.startsWith('/api/commands/')){
    const a=await actor(req);if(req.headers['content-type']?.split(';')[0]!=='application/json')fail('UNSUPPORTED_TYPE',415);
    let value;try{value=JSON.parse((await readBody(req,64*1024)).toString('utf8'));}catch(e){if(e instanceof PlatformError)throw e;fail('INVALID_INPUT');}
    const operation=decodeURIComponent(url.pathname.slice(14));const result=await app.execute(a,operation,value,req.headers['idempotency-key']);
    if(operation==='account.delete')await media.cleanupDeletedOwners();json(res,200,result);return;
   }
   if(serveIntegrations){if(req.method==='POST'&&url.pathname.startsWith('/api/extract/'))await app.privateQuery(await actor(req),'profile');if(await serveIntegrations(req,res,url))return;}
   if(serveStatic&&await serveStatic(req,res,url))return;
   fail('NOT_FOUND',404);
  }catch(e){if(res.destroyed||res.headersSent)return;const known=Number.isInteger(e.status)&&e.status>=400&&e.status<600;json(res,known?e.status:500,{error:known?e.code:'INTERNAL',requestId});}
 });server.requestTimeout=15000;server.headersTimeout=10000;return server;
}

// Reuse the reviewed fixed-host map proxy and bounded local extractor.
import {createAdminPreviewServer} from '../../START_ADMIN_CONTENT_VISUAL_2026-10-07/server.mjs';
export function createIntegrations(options={}){
 const existing=createAdminPreviewServer(options);
 return async(req,res,url)=>{
  if(!url.pathname.startsWith('/api/map/')&&!url.pathname.startsWith('/_AMapService/')&&!url.pathname.startsWith('/api/extract/'))return false;
  existing.emit('request',req,res);return true;
 };
}

import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {fail} from './contracts.mjs';
export async function exchangeContext(ctx,id){
 const r=await ctx.one('SELECT e.*,l.user_id AS publisher_id FROM start_app.exchanges e JOIN start_app.listings l ON l.id=e.listing_id WHERE e.id=$1',[id]);
 if(![r.publisher_id,r.requester_id].includes(ctx.userId))fail('FORBIDDEN',403);return r;
}
export async function marketCommand(ctx,op,v){
 const {client,userId,one,content,owns,notify}=ctx;
 if(op==='listing.create'){
  const c=await one("SELECT * FROM start_app.communities WHERE id=$1 AND status='active'",[v.communityId]);if(c.artist_id!==v.artistId)fail('CONFLICT',409);await content(v.artistId,'artist');
  await one("SELECT 1 FROM start_app.memberships WHERE community_id=$1 AND user_id=$2 AND status='joined'",[c.id,userId]);
  let placeId=v.placeId;if(v.eventId){const e=await content(v.eventId,'event');if(!e.published.artistIds.includes(v.artistId)||(placeId&&placeId!==e.published.placeId))fail('CONFLICT',409);placeId=e.published.placeId;}if(placeId)await content(placeId,'place');await ctx.media(v.mediaIds);
  const id=randomUUID();await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.listings(id,user_id,community_id,artist_id,event_id,place_id,data,status) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,$7,$8)',[id,userId,c.id,v.artistId,v.eventId??null,placeId??null,JSON.stringify({...v,...(placeId?{placeId}:{})}),v.status]);
  if(v.mediaIds?.length)await client.query("UPDATE start_app.media SET visibility='public' WHERE id=ANY($1::uuid[])",[v.mediaIds]);return {id};
 }
 if(op==='listing.status'){owns(await one('SELECT * FROM start_app.listings WHERE id=$1',[v.id]));await client.query('UPDATE start_app.listings SET status=$2 WHERE id=$1',[v.id,v.status]);return {id:v.id,status:v.status};}
 if(op==='listing.edit'){
  const l=await one('SELECT * FROM start_app.listings WHERE id=$1',[v.id]);owns(l);await ctx.media(v.mediaIds);
  const data={...l.data,name:v.name,description:v.description,...(v.mediaIds?{mediaIds:v.mediaIds}:{})};
  await client.query('UPDATE start_app.listings SET data=$2 WHERE id=$1',[v.id,JSON.stringify(data)]);
  if(v.mediaIds?.length)await client.query("UPDATE start_app.media SET visibility='public' WHERE id=ANY($1::uuid[])",[v.mediaIds]);return {id:v.id};
 }
 if(op==='listing.comment'){
  const l=await one("SELECT l.* FROM start_app.listings l JOIN start_app.communities c ON c.id=l.community_id JOIN start_accounts.users u ON u.id=l.user_id WHERE l.id=$1 AND l.status='available' AND c.status='active' AND u.status='active'",[v.listingId]);
  const id=randomUUID();await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.listing_comments(id,listing_id,user_id,text) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4)',[id,l.id,userId,v.text]);return {id};
 }
 if(op==='listing.comment.delete'){
  const comment=await one('SELECT * FROM start_app.listing_comments WHERE id=$1',[v.id]);
  if(comment.user_id!==userId)await ctx.admin();await client.query('DELETE FROM start_app.listing_comments WHERE id=$1',[v.id]);return {id:v.id};
 }
 if(op==='exchange.apply'){
  const l=await one("SELECT * FROM start_app.listings WHERE id=$1 AND status='available'",[v.listingId]);if(l.user_id===userId)fail('CONFLICT',409);
  const old=(await client.query('SELECT * FROM start_app.exchanges WHERE listing_id=$1 AND requester_id=$2',[l.id,userId])).rows[0];if(old){if(old.status==='withdrawn')fail('CONFLICT',409);return {id:old.id};}
  const id=randomUUID();await client.query("INSERT INTO start_app.exchanges(id,listing_id,requester_id,status) VALUES($1,$2,$3,'pending')",[id,l.id,userId]);await notify(l.user_id,'exchange.requested',id);return {id};
 }
 if(op==='exchange.accept'){const e=await exchangeContext(ctx,v.id);if(e.publisher_id!==userId)fail('FORBIDDEN',403);if(e.status==='withdrawn')fail('CONFLICT',409);if(e.status==='pending'){await client.query("UPDATE start_app.exchanges SET status='accepted' WHERE id=$1",[e.id]);await notify(e.requester_id,'exchange.accepted',e.id);}return {id:e.id,status:'accepted'};}
 if(op==='exchange.withdraw'){const e=await exchangeContext(ctx,v.id);await client.query("UPDATE start_app.exchanges SET status='withdrawn' WHERE id=$1",[e.id]);await client.query('DELETE FROM start_app.contacts WHERE exchange_id=$1',[e.id]);return {id:e.id};}
 return null;
}

FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/src/media.mjs

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import {randomUUID,createHash} from 'node:crypto';
import {createRequire} from 'node:module';
import {uuid,fail} from './contracts.mjs';
import {publiclyReferenced,reviewableMedia} from './public-media.mjs';
const require=createRequire(new URL('../../../.worktrees/start-slice-001/package.json',import.meta.url));
const {PNG}=require('pngjs');
const maxBytes=5*1024*1024;
export function createMedia(pool,rootUrl){
 const root=path.resolve(rootUrl instanceof URL?fileURLToPath(rootUrl):rootUrl);
 const filePath=name=>{if(!/^[0-9a-f-]{36}\.png$/.test(name))fail('INVALID_MEDIA');const p=path.resolve(root,name);if(path.dirname(p)!==root)fail('INVALID_MEDIA');return p;};
 async function active(c,actor){if(!actor)fail('UNAUTHENTICATED',401);uuid(actor.id);if(!(await c.query("SELECT 1 FROM start_accounts.users WHERE id=$1 AND status='active' FOR UPDATE",[actor.id])).rowCount)fail('FORBIDDEN',403);}
 async function transaction(work){const c=await pool.connect();try{await c.query('BEGIN');await c.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(710082026)');const r=await work(c);await c.query('COMMIT');return r;}catch(e){await c.query('ROLLBACK');throw e;}finally{c.release();}}
 return {
  async upload(actor,bytes,mime){
   if(mime!=='image/png'||!Buffer.isBuffer(bytes)||!bytes.length||bytes.length>maxBytes||bytes.length<33||!bytes.subarray(0,8).equals(Buffer.from([137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10])))fail('INVALID_MEDIA');
   if(bytes.readUInt32BE(16)>4096||bytes.readUInt32BE(20)>4096||bytes.readUInt32BE(16)*bytes.readUInt32BE(20)>16_000_000)fail('INVALID_MEDIA');
   try{PNG.sync.read(bytes,{checkCRC:true});}catch{fail('INVALID_MEDIA');}
   const id=randomUUID(),name=id+'.png',sha=createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');await fs.mkdir(root,{recursive:true});
   return transaction(async c=>{await active(c,actor);await fs.writeFile(filePath(name),bytes,{flag:'wx'});try{await c.query('INSERT INTO start_app.media(id,user_id,mime,size,sha256,path) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6)',[id,actor.id,mime,bytes.length,sha,name]);}catch(e){await fs.unlink(filePath(name));throw e;}return {id,mime,size:bytes.length};});
  },
  async read(actor,id){uuid(id);return transaction(async c=>{const row=(await c.query('SELECT * FROM start_app.media WHERE id=$1',[id])).rows[0];if(!row)fail('NOT_FOUND',404);if(row.visibility!=='public'||!await publiclyReferenced(c,id)){await active(c,actor);if(actor.id!==row.user_id){const admin=(await c.query('SELECT 1 FROM start_app.admins WHERE user_id=$1',[actor.id])).rowCount;if(!admin||!await reviewableMedia(c,id))fail('FORBIDDEN',403);}}const p=filePath(row.path),s=await fs.lstat(p);if(!s.isFile()||s.isSymbolicLink())fail('NOT_FOUND',404);const bytes=await fs.readFile(p);if(createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex')!==row.sha256)fail('MEDIA_UNAVAILABLE',503);return {bytes,mime:row.mime};});},
  async cleanupDeletedOwners(){return transaction(async c=>{const rows=(await c.query("SELECT m.* FROM start_app.media m JOIN start_accounts.users u ON u.id=m.user_id WHERE u.status='disabled' AND m.visibility='private'")).rows;for(const r of rows){try{await fs.unlink(filePath(r.path));}catch(e){if(e.code!=='ENOENT')throw e;}await c.query('DELETE FROM start_app.media WHERE id=$1',[r.id]);}return {removed:rows.length};});},
 };
}

import {randomUUID,randomBytes,createHash} from 'node:crypto';
import {fail} from './contracts.mjs';
import {validateProfilePatch} from '../../START_ACCOUNT_FOUNDATION_2026-10-05/src/accounts.mjs';
export const hash=s=>createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
export async function personalCommand(ctx,op,v){
 const {client,userId,one,owns,content}=ctx;
 if(op==='profile.update'){const patch=validateProfilePatch(v),current=await one('SELECT * FROM start_accounts.profiles WHERE user_id=$1',[userId]),next={...current,...patch,privacy:{...current.privacy,...patch.privacy}};await client.query('UPDATE start_accounts.profiles SET nickname=$2,artists=$3,interests=$4,privacy=$5,updated_at=now() WHERE user_id=$1',[userId,next.nickname,next.artists,next.interests,JSON.stringify(next.privacy)]);return {nickname:next.nickname,artists:next.artists,interests:next.interests,privacy:next.privacy};}
 if(op==='checkin.create'){
  if(Date.parse(v.at)>Date.now())fail('INVALID_INPUT');const p=await content(v.placeId,'place');await ctx.media(v.mediaIds);const id=randomUUID();
  const snapshot={id:p.id,name:p.published.name,city:p.published.city,category:p.published.category};
  await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.checkins(id,user_id,place_id,at,snapshot,text,media_ids,ticket) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,$5,$6,$7,$8)',[id,userId,p.id,v.at,JSON.stringify(snapshot),v.text,v.mediaIds,v.ticket?JSON.stringify(v.ticket):null]);return {id};
 }
 if(op==='checkin.delete'){owns(await one('SELECT user_id FROM start_app.checkins WHERE id=$1',[v.id]));await client.query('UPDATE start_app.shares SET revoked=true WHERE $1=ANY(record_ids)',[v.id]);await client.query('DELETE FROM start_app.checkins WHERE id=$1',[v.id]);return {id:v.id};}
 if(op==='share.create'){
  for(const id of v.ids)owns(await one('SELECT user_id FROM start_app.checkins WHERE id=$1',[id]));
  const id=randomUUID(),token=randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.shares(id,user_id,token_hash,record_ids,fields) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,$5)',[id,userId,hash(token),v.ids,v.fields]);return {id,token};
 }
 if(op==='share.revoke'){owns(await one('SELECT user_id FROM start_app.shares WHERE id=$1',[v.id]));await client.query('UPDATE start_app.shares SET revoked=true WHERE id=$1',[v.id]);return {id:v.id};}
 if(op==='doll.choose'){const current=(await client.query('SELECT * FROM start_app.dolls WHERE user_id=$1',[userId])).rows[0];if(current){if(current.character!==v.character)fail('CONFLICT',409);return current;}await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.dolls(user_id,character) VALUES($1,$2)',[userId,v.character]);return {character:v.character,outfit:'original',expression:'neutral'};}
 if(op==='doll.style'){await one('UPDATE start_app.dolls SET outfit=COALESCE($2,outfit),expression=COALESCE($3,expression) WHERE user_id=$1 RETURNING user_id',[userId,v.outfit??null,v.expression??null]);return {ok:true};}
 if(op==='favorite.set'){await one('SELECT id FROM start_app.content WHERE id=$1 AND published IS NOT NULL',[v.id]);if(v.enabled)await client.query('INSERT INTO start_app.favorites(user_id,content_id) VALUES($1,$2) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING',[userId,v.id]);else await client.query('DELETE FROM start_app.favorites WHERE user_id=$1 AND content_id=$2',[userId,v.id]);return {id:v.id,enabled:v.enabled};}
 if(op==='notification.read'){await one('UPDATE start_app.notifications SET read=true WHERE id=$1 AND user_id=$2 RETURNING id',[v.id,userId]);return {id:v.id};}
 if(op==='account.delete'){
  await client.query('DELETE FROM start_app.listing_comments WHERE user_id=$1',[userId]);
  await client.query('DELETE FROM start_app.contacts WHERE user_id=$1 OR exchange_id IN (SELECT e.id FROM start_app.exchanges e JOIN start_app.listings l ON l.id=e.listing_id WHERE e.requester_id=$1 OR l.user_id=$1)',[userId]);
  await client.query("UPDATE start_app.exchanges SET status='withdrawn' WHERE requester_id=$1 OR listing_id IN (SELECT id FROM start_app.listings WHERE user_id=$1)",[userId]);
  await client.query("UPDATE start_app.listings SET status='withdrawn',data=data-'meeting' WHERE user_id=$1",[userId]);
  for(const table of ['shares','checkins','dolls','favorites','notifications','idempotency'])await client.query(`DELETE FROM start_app.${table} WHERE user_id=$1`,[userId]);
  await client.query("UPDATE start_app.media SET visibility='private' WHERE user_id=$1 AND visibility='private'",[userId]);
  await client.query('DELETE FROM start_app.memberships WHERE user_id=$1',[userId]);
  await client.query('UPDATE start_app.communities SET manager_id=NULL WHERE manager_id=$1',[userId]);
  await client.query("UPDATE start_accounts.profiles SET nickname='已注销',artists='',interests='' WHERE user_id=$1",[userId]);
  await client.query("UPDATE start_accounts.users SET status='disabled' WHERE id=$1",[userId]);
  return {ok:true};
 }
 return null;
}

FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/src/platform.mjs

import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';
import {parse,uuid,fail} from './contracts.mjs';
import {context} from './context.mjs';
import {contentCommand} from './content.mjs';
import {communityCommand} from './community.mjs';
import {marketCommand} from './market.mjs';
import {personalCommand,hash} from './personal.mjs';
import {publiclyReferenced,reviewableMedia} from './public-media.mjs';
export function createPlatform(pool){
 async function transaction(fn){const c=await pool.connect();try{await c.query('BEGIN');await c.query("SET LOCAL statement_timeout='8s'");const result=await fn(c);await c.query('COMMIT');return result;}catch(e){await c.query('ROLLBACK');if(e.code==='23505')fail('CONFLICT',409);throw e;}finally{c.release();}}
 async function active(c,actor){if(!actor)fail('UNAUTHENTICATED',401);uuid(actor.id);const r=await c.query("SELECT id FROM start_accounts.users WHERE id=$1 AND status='active' FOR UPDATE",[actor.id]);if(!r.rowCount)fail('FORBIDDEN',403);return context(c,actor.id);}
 async function publicQuery(filters={}){
  const allowed=['artistId','city','category'];if(Object.keys(filters).some(k=>!allowed.includes(k)))fail('INVALID_INPUT');if(filters.artistId)uuid(filters.artistId);for(const k of ['city','category'])if(filters[k]&&(typeof filters[k]!=='string'||filters[k].length>200))fail('INVALID_INPUT');
  return transaction(async c=>{
   await c.query('SET TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL REPEATABLE READ');
   const rows=(await c.query('SELECT id,kind,published FROM start_app.content WHERE published IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id')).rows;
   const result={artists:[],places:[],routes:[],events:[],communities:[],listings:[]};
   const relations=(await c.query('SELECT place_id,artist_id,data FROM start_app.contributions')).rows;
   for(const r of rows){const data={id:r.id,...r.published};if(r.kind==='place')data.artistIds=[...new Set([...relations.filter(x=>x.place_id===r.id).map(x=>x.artist_id),...(data.artistEvidence?[data.artistEvidence.artistId]:[])])];
    const artists=r.kind==='artist'?[r.id]:r.kind==='place'?data.artistIds:r.kind==='route'?[data.artistId]:data.artistIds;
    if(filters.artistId&&!artists.includes(filters.artistId))continue;
    if(filters.city&&r.kind!=='artist'&&data.city!==filters.city)continue;
    if(filters.category&&r.kind==='place'&&data.category!==filters.category)continue;
    result[{artist:'artists',place:'places',route:'routes',event:'events'}[r.kind]].push(data);
   }
   result.communities=(await c.query("SELECT id,artist_id,manager_id,name,description,notice FROM start_app.communities WHERE status='active' ORDER BY id")).rows.filter(r=>!filters.artistId||r.artist_id===filters.artistId);
   result.listings=(await c.query("SELECT l.* FROM start_app.listings l JOIN start_app.communities c ON c.id=l.community_id JOIN start_accounts.users u ON u.id=l.user_id WHERE l.status='available' AND c.status='active' AND u.status='active' ORDER BY l.id")).rows.filter(r=>(!filters.artistId||r.artist_id===filters.artistId)&&(!filters.city||rows.some(p=>p.id===r.place_id&&p.published.city===filters.city))).map(r=>({id:r.id,communityId:r.community_id,artistId:r.artist_id,eventId:r.event_id,placeId:r.place_id,name:r.data.name,description:r.data.description,status:r.status,mediaIds:r.data.mediaIds||[]}));
   return result;
  });
 }
 return {
  pool,
  async execute(actor,op,input,key){const v=parse(op,input);if(typeof key!=='string'||!key||key.length>100)fail('INVALID_INPUT');return transaction(async c=>{
   // Local acceptance deliberately serializes mutations. A production adapter can narrow locks after measuring load.
   await c.query('SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(710082026)');const ctx=await active(c,actor),fingerprint=hash(JSON.stringify(v));
   const old=(await c.query('SELECT fingerprint,result FROM start_app.idempotency WHERE user_id=$1 AND operation=$2 AND key=$3',[ctx.userId,op,key])).rows[0];if(old){if(old.fingerprint!==fingerprint)fail('CONFLICT',409);return old.result;}
   let result=null;for(const fn of [contentCommand,communityCommand,marketCommand,personalCommand]){result=await fn(ctx,op,v);if(result!==null)break;}if(result===null)fail('INVALID_INPUT');
   await c.query('INSERT INTO start_app.idempotency(user_id,operation,key,fingerprint,result) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4,$5)',[ctx.userId,op,key,fingerprint,JSON.stringify(result)]);
   await c.query('INSERT INTO start_app.audit(id,user_id,operation,reference_id) VALUES($1,$2,$3,$4)',[randomUUID(),ctx.userId,op,result.id||null]);return result;
  });},
  publicQuery,
  async home(){const all=await publicQuery(),ids=(await pool.query("SELECT data FROM start_app.settings WHERE key='home'")).rows[0]?.data.ids||[],records=[...all.places,...all.routes,...all.events];return {featured:ids.map(id=>records.find(r=>r.id===id)).filter(Boolean)};},
  async publicDetail(kind,id){uuid(id);if(kind==='listing'){const all=await publicQuery();const l=all.listings.find(r=>r.id===id);if(!l)fail('NOT_FOUND',404);const comments=(await pool.query("SELECT c.id,c.text,c.created_at,COALESCE(p.nickname,'START 用户') AS author FROM start_app.listing_comments c JOIN start_accounts.users u ON u.id=c.user_id LEFT JOIN start_accounts.profiles p ON p.user_id=c.user_id WHERE c.listing_id=$1 AND u.status='active' ORDER BY c.created_at,c.id",[id])).rows;return {...l,comments};}if(!['artist','place','route','event'].includes(kind))fail('INVALID_INPUT');const all=await publicQuery();const section={artist:'artists',place:'places',route:'routes',event:'events'}[kind],record=all[section].find(r=>r.id===id);if(!record)fail('NOT_FOUND',404);
   if(kind==='place'){record.contributions=(await pool.query('SELECT artist_id,data FROM start_app.contributions WHERE place_id=$1 ORDER BY id',[id])).rows.map(r=>({artistId:r.artist_id,source:r.data.source,sameSpot:r.data.sameSpot,mediaIds:r.data.mediaIds}));if(record.artistEvidence)record.contributions.push(record.artistEvidence);record.routes=all.routes.filter(r=>r.placeIds.includes(id));}
   if(kind==='artist'){record.places=all.places.filter(r=>r.artistIds.includes(id));record.routes=all.routes.filter(r=>r.artistId===id);record.events=all.events.filter(r=>r.artistIds.includes(id));}return record;
  },
  async adminQuery(actor,section,id){return transaction(async c=>{const ctx=await active(c,actor);await ctx.admin();if(section==='media'){const rows=(await c.query('SELECT id,user_id,visibility FROM start_app.media ORDER BY id')).rows,result=[];for(const r of rows)if(r.user_id===ctx.userId||(r.visibility==='public'&&await publiclyReferenced(c,r.id))||await reviewableMedia(c,r.id))result.push({id:r.id,visibility:r.visibility});return result;}const queries={content:'SELECT id,kind,version,draft,published FROM start_app.content ORDER BY updated_at DESC',submissions:'SELECT * FROM start_app.submissions ORDER BY id',applications:'SELECT * FROM start_app.community_applications ORDER BY id',requests:'SELECT * FROM start_app.community_requests ORDER BY id',audit:'SELECT * FROM start_app.audit ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 200',revisions:'SELECT version,data FROM start_app.revisions WHERE content_id=$1 ORDER BY version'};if(!queries[section])fail('INVALID_INPUT');if(section==='revisions')uuid(id);return (await c.query(queries[section],section==='revisions'?[id]:[])).rows;});},
  async privateQuery(actor,section){return transaction(async c=>{
   const ctx=await active(c,actor);
   const queries={book:'SELECT id,place_id,at,snapshot,text,media_ids,ticket FROM start_app.checkins WHERE user_id=$1 ORDER BY at,id',submissions:'SELECT id,artist_id,data,status,reason,place_id FROM start_app.submissions WHERE user_id=$1 ORDER BY id',memberships:"SELECT m.community_id,m.status,c.artist_id FROM start_app.memberships m JOIN start_app.communities c ON c.id=m.community_id WHERE m.user_id=$1 AND m.status!='removed' AND c.status='active'",notifications:'SELECT id,kind,reference_id,read,created_at FROM start_app.notifications WHERE user_id=$1 ORDER BY created_at,id',doll:'SELECT character,outfit,expression FROM start_app.dolls WHERE user_id=$1',favorites:'SELECT content_id FROM start_app.favorites WHERE user_id=$1',listingComments:'SELECT id,listing_id FROM start_app.listing_comments WHERE user_id=$1',exchanges:"SELECT e.id,e.listing_id,e.status,l.data->>'name' AS name,(l.user_id=$1) AS publisher FROM start_app.exchanges e JOIN start_app.listings l ON l.id=e.listing_id WHERE e.requester_id=$1 OR l.user_id=$1",listings:'SELECT id,data,status FROM start_app.listings WHERE user_id=$1',profile:'SELECT nickname,artists,interests,privacy FROM start_accounts.profiles WHERE user_id=$1'};
   queries.shares='SELECT id,record_ids,fields,revoked FROM start_app.shares WHERE user_id=$1 ORDER BY id';
   if(!queries[section])fail('INVALID_INPUT');const data=(await c.query(queries[section],[ctx.userId])).rows;return ['doll','profile'].includes(section)?data[0]||null:data;
  });},

  async shareMediaOwner(token,id){uuid(id);if(typeof token!=='string'||!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(token))fail('NOT_FOUND',404);return transaction(async c=>{const s=(await c.query("SELECT s.* FROM start_app.shares s JOIN start_accounts.users u ON u.id=s.user_id WHERE s.token_hash=$1 AND NOT s.revoked AND u.status='active'",[hash(token)])).rows[0];if(!s||!s.fields.includes('media_ids'))fail('NOT_FOUND',404);const found=await c.query('SELECT 1 FROM start_app.checkins WHERE id=ANY($1::uuid[]) AND user_id=$2 AND $3=ANY(media_ids)',[s.record_ids,s.user_id,id]);if(!found.rowCount)fail('NOT_FOUND',404);return {id:s.user_id};});},
  async readShare(token){if(typeof token!=='string'||!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(token))fail('NOT_FOUND',404);return transaction(async c=>{
   const s=(await c.query("SELECT s.* FROM start_app.shares s JOIN start_accounts.users u ON u.id=s.user_id WHERE s.token_hash=$1 AND NOT s.revoked AND u.status='active'",[hash(token)])).rows[0];if(!s)fail('NOT_FOUND',404);
   const data=(await c.query('SELECT * FROM start_app.checkins WHERE id=ANY($1::uuid[]) AND user_id=$2',[s.record_ids,s.user_id])).rows;return s.record_ids.map(id=>{const row=data.find(r=>r.id===id);if(!row)fail('NOT_FOUND',404);return Object.fromEntries(s.fields.map(f=>[f,row[f]]));});
  });},
  async recommend(actor,{city}={}){if(typeof city!=='string'||!city.trim()||city.length>200)fail('INVALID_INPUT');const joined=actor?await this.privateQuery(actor,'memberships'):[],profile=actor?await this.privateQuery(actor,'profile'):null,preferred=new Set(joined.map(r=>r.artist_id));
   const data=await publicQuery({city});const rank=r=>(r.artistIds||[r.artistId]).some(id=>preferred.has(id))?2:0;
   const interests=profile?.interests?.split(/[、,，\s]+/).filter(Boolean)||[];const genres=profile?.artists?.split(/[、,，\s]+/).filter(Boolean)||[];
   for(const section of ['places','routes','events'])data[section].sort((a,b)=>(rank(b)+interests.filter(x=>[b.category,b.name].some(s=>s?.includes(x))).length+genres.filter(x=>b.name?.includes(x)).length)-(rank(a)+interests.filter(x=>[a.category,a.name].some(s=>s?.includes(x))).length+genres.filter(x=>a.name?.includes(x)).length)||a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
   data.events=data.events.filter(e=>['upcoming','ongoing'].includes(e.status)&&Date.parse(e.startsAt)>=Date.now()-86400000);return {city,...data};
  },
 };
}

// Visibility is a consent marker; current public references determine anonymous access.
export async function publiclyReferenced(client,id){
 const r=await client.query(`SELECT 1 WHERE EXISTS (
 SELECT 1 FROM start_app.content WHERE published IS NOT NULL AND (published->'mediaIds') ? $1
 ) OR EXISTS (
 SELECT 1 FROM start_app.contributions x JOIN start_app.content p ON p.id=x.place_id JOIN start_app.content a ON a.id=x.artist_id WHERE p.published IS NOT NULL AND a.published IS NOT NULL AND (x.data->'mediaIds') ? $1
 ) OR EXISTS (
 SELECT 1 FROM start_app.listings l JOIN start_app.communities c ON c.id=l.community_id JOIN start_accounts.users u ON u.id=l.user_id WHERE l.status='available' AND c.status='active' AND u.status='active' AND (l.data->'mediaIds') ? $1
 )`,[id]);return r.rowCount>0;
}
export async function reviewableMedia(client,id){const r=await client.query(`SELECT 1 WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM start_app.submissions s JOIN start_accounts.users u ON u.id=s.user_id WHERE s.status='pending' AND u.status='active' AND (s.data->'mediaIds') ? $1) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM start_app.corrections c JOIN start_accounts.users u ON u.id=c.user_id WHERE c.status='pending' AND u.status='active' AND (c.data->'data'->'mediaIds') ? $1)`,[id]);return r.rowCount>0;}

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
const dir=path.resolve(fileURLToPath(new URL('../',import.meta.url))),original=path.resolve(fileURLToPath(new URL('../../START_ADMIN_CONTENT_VISUAL_2026-10-07/',import.meta.url)));
const types={'.js':'text/javascript; charset=utf-8','.mjs':'text/javascript; charset=utf-8','.css':'text/css; charset=utf-8','.html':'text/html; charset=utf-8','.png':'image/png','.jpg':'image/jpeg','.svg':'image/svg+xml'};
const approved=path.resolve(fileURLToPath(new URL('../../START_FINAL_LAYOUT_REVIEW_2026-10-06/',import.meta.url)));
export async function serveStatic(req,res,url){
 if(req.method!=='GET')return false;
 let file;
 if(url.pathname==='/client.mjs')file=path.join(dir,'client.mjs');
 else if(url.pathname.startsWith('/app/')||url.pathname.startsWith('/prototype/')){
  const prototype=url.pathname.startsWith('/prototype/'),name=decodeURIComponent(url.pathname.slice(prototype?11:5))||'index.html';
  if(name.split('/').some(s=>s==='..'||s==='.'||s.includes('\\'))||!types[path.extname(name)])return false;
  const local=path.resolve(dir,'public-ui',name),fallback=path.resolve(approved,name);
  if(!local.startsWith(path.join(dir,'public-ui')+path.sep)||!fallback.startsWith(approved+path.sep))return false;
  if(prototype)file=fallback;else{try{await fs.access(local);file=local;}catch{if(!/^(assets\/|vendor\/embla\.js$|[a-z-]+\.(css|mjs)$)/.test(name))return false;file=fallback;}}
 }
 else if(url.pathname.startsWith('/admin/')){let name=decodeURIComponent(url.pathname.slice(7))||'index.html';if(name.split('/').some(s=>s==='..'||s==='.'||s.includes('\\'))||!types[path.extname(name)])return false;const local=path.resolve(dir,'ui',name),fallback=path.resolve(original,name);if(!local.startsWith(path.join(dir,'ui')+path.sep)||!fallback.startsWith(original+path.sep))return false;try{await fs.access(local);file=local;}catch{if(!/^(assets\/|map-picker\.(html|js|css)$|text-import\.(html|js|css)$|batch-domain\.js$)/.test(name))return false;file=fallback;}}
 else return false;
 try{const bytes=await fs.readFile(file);res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':types[path.extname(file)],'cache-control':'no-store','x-content-type-options':'nosniff'});res.end(bytes);return true;}catch{return false;}
}

FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/tests/backup-acceptance.mjs

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import path from 'node:path';import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';import {execFileSync} from 'node:child_process';import {randomUUID,createHash} from 'node:crypto';import assert from 'node:assert/strict';import {Pool} from '../src/runtime.mjs';
const root=fileURLToPath(new URL('../',import.meta.url)),bin=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../integration-runtime/postgresql-18.6/pgsql/bin/',import.meta.url));
const source=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(path.join(root,'evidence','BROWSER_ACCEPTANCE.json'),'utf8')).database;
assert.match(source,/^start_test_browser_[a-f0-9]{32}$/);
const database='start_test_restore_'+randomUUID().replaceAll('-',''),out=path.join(root,'evidence','backups',database);await fs.mkdir(out,{recursive:true});const archive=path.join(out,'database.dump');
const args=['-h','127.0.0.1','-p','55435','-U','start_local'];execFileSync(path.join(bin,'pg_dump.exe'),[...args,'-Fc','-f',archive,source],{stdio:'pipe'});execFileSync(path.join(bin,'createdb.exe'),[...args,database],{stdio:'pipe'});execFileSync(path.join(bin,'pg_restore.exe'),[...args,'--exit-on-error','-d',database,archive],{stdio:'pipe'});
const open=db=>new Pool({host:'127.0.0.1',port:55435,user:'start_local',database:db});const original=open(source),restored=open(database);
try{
 const tables=(await original.query("SELECT table_schema,table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema IN ('start_app','start_accounts') AND table_type='BASE TABLE' ORDER BY table_schema,table_name")).rows,checks=[];
 for(const t of tables){assert.match(t.table_name,/^[a-z_]+$/);const sql=`SELECT row_to_json(t) AS row FROM ${t.table_schema}.${t.table_name} t`;const a=(await original.query(sql)).rows.map(r=>JSON.stringify(r.row)).sort(),b=(await restored.query(sql)).rows.map(r=>JSON.stringify(r.row)).sort();assert.deepEqual(b,a);checks.push({table:t.table_schema+'.'+t.table_name,rows:a.length});}
 const images=(await original.query('SELECT id,path,sha256 FROM start_app.media')).rows;const mediaDir=path.join(root,'data','media-browser');await fs.mkdir(path.join(out,'media'),{recursive:true});for(const image of images){assert.match(image.path,/^[a-f0-9-]{36}\.png$/);const bytes=await fs.readFile(path.join(mediaDir,image.path));assert.equal(createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex'),image.sha256);await fs.writeFile(path.join(out,'media',image.path),bytes);assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(path.join(out,'media',image.path)),bytes);}
 await fs.writeFile(path.join(root,'evidence','BACKUP_ACCEPTANCE.json'),JSON.stringify({source,restored:database,archive,archiveSha256:createHash('sha256').update(await fs.readFile(archive)).digest('hex'),tables:checks,mediaFiles:images.length,oldDatabasesUntouched:true},null,2));console.log('PASS restore',checks.length,'tables and',images.length,'media files');
}finally{await original.end();await restored.end();}

import test from 'node:test';import assert from 'node:assert/strict';import fs from 'node:fs/promises';import {randomUUID} from 'node:crypto';import {Pool} from '../src/runtime.mjs';import {createPlatform} from '../src/platform.mjs';import {createServer} from '../src/http.mjs';import {createMedia} from '../src/media.mjs';import {createPlatformClient} from '../client.mjs';import {createProfileRepository} from '../../START_ACCOUNT_FOUNDATION_2026-10-05/src/profile-repository.mjs';
test('client adapter joins upload → moderation → artist/city discovery → route/event → community/market → private Book',async()=>{
 const database='start_test_client_'+randomUUID().replaceAll('-',''),setup=new Pool({host:'127.0.0.1',port:55435,user:'start_local',database:'postgres'});await setup.query(`CREATE DATABASE ${database}`);await setup.end();const pool=new Pool({host:'127.0.0.1',port:55435,user:'start_local',database});let server;
 try{
  for(const file of ['../../START_ACCOUNT_FOUNDATION_2026-10-05/db/001_accounts.sql','../db/001_platform.sql'])await pool.query(await fs.readFile(new URL(file,import.meta.url),'utf8'));const repo=createProfileRepository(pool);const adminId=await repo.resolveIdentity({provider:'test_client',subject:'admin'});await pool.query('INSERT INTO start_app.admins(user_id) VALUES($1)',[adminId]);server=createServer({app:createPlatform(pool),repository:repo,media:createMedia(pool,new URL('../data/media-client/'+database+'/',import.meta.url)),verifyBearer:token=>['admin','alice','bob'].includes(token)?{provider:'test_client',subject:token}:null});await new Promise(r=>server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1',r));const base='http://127.0.0.1:'+server.address().port,client=name=>createPlatformClient({base,getBearer:async()=>name}),admin=client('admin'),alice=client('alice'),bob=client('bob'),anonymous=client(null);
  const artist=await admin.command('artist.save',{name:'测试安溥',genres:['流行']});await admin.command('content.publish',{id:artist.id,version:1});
  const submission=await alice.command('submission.create',{artistId:artist.id,name:'上海试验咖啡馆',city:'上海',category:'咖啡',source:'测试原文',poiId:'CLIENT_FIXTURE_ONLY',sameSpot:'照片机位'});assert.equal((await anonymous.public({city:'上海'})).places.length,0);const point=await admin.command('submission.review',{id:submission.id,decision:'approve',checked:true});assert.equal((await alice.me('submissions'))[0].status,'approved');
  assert.equal((await anonymous.public({artistId:artist.id,city:'上海',category:'咖啡'})).places[0].id,point.id);assert.equal((await anonymous.detail('place',point.id)).contributions[0].sameSpot,'照片机位');
  const route=await alice.command('routeSubmission.create',{name:'上海半日路线',city:'上海',artistId:artist.id,placeIds:[point.id],source:'测试整理'});const routeRecord=await admin.command('submission.review',{id:route.id,decision:'approve',checked:true});assert.equal((await anonymous.detail('artist',artist.id)).routes[0].id,routeRecord.id);
  const event=await admin.command('event.save',{name:'测试演出',city:'上海',artistIds:[artist.id],placeId:point.id,startsAt:'2026-10-09T12:00:00Z',timeZone:'Asia/Shanghai',status:'upcoming',source:'测试资料'});await admin.command('content.publish',{id:event.id,version:1});
  const application=await alice.command('community.apply',{artistId:artist.id,name:'测试安溥社群'}),community=await admin.command('community.review',{id:application.id,decision:'approve'});await bob.command('community.join',{id:community.id});assert.equal((await bob.me('memberships'))[0].community_id,community.id);
  const listing=await alice.command('listing.create',{communityId:community.id,artistId:artist.id,eventId:event.id,placeId:point.id,name:'明信片',status:'available',meeting:'不公开的集合说明'});assert.equal((await anonymous.public({city:'上海'})).listings[0].meeting,undefined);const exchange=await bob.command('exchange.apply',{listingId:listing.id});await alice.command('exchange.accept',{id:exchange.id});const comment=await bob.command('listing.comment',{listingId:listing.id,text:'评论自行沟通'});assert.equal((await anonymous.detail('listing',listing.id)).comments[0].text,'评论自行沟通');await bob.command('listing.comment.delete',{id:comment.id});assert.equal((await anonymous.detail('listing',listing.id)).comments.length,0);
  const record=await bob.command('checkin.create',{placeId:point.id,at:'2026-10-01T00:00:00Z',text:'私密内容'});assert.equal((await bob.me('book'))[0].id,record.id);assert.equal((await alice.me('book')).length,0);const share=await bob.command('share.create',{ids:[record.id],fields:['snapshot']});assert.equal((await anonymous.share(share.token))[0].text,undefined);await bob.command('share.revoke',{id:share.id});await assert.rejects(()=>anonymous.share(share.token),e=>e.status===404);
  await bob.command('doll.choose',{character:'boy'});await bob.command('doll.style',{outfit:'outfit5',expression:'smile'});assert.equal((await bob.me('doll')).outfit,'outfit5');await assert.rejects(()=>bob.command('doll.choose',{character:'girl'}),e=>e.status===409);await alice.command('listing.status',{id:listing.id,status:'withdrawn'});assert.equal((await anonymous.public()).listings.length,0);
  await fs.writeFile(new URL('../evidence/CLIENT_WORKFLOW.json',import.meta.url),JSON.stringify({database,testIdentityOnly:true,realWechat:false,steps:['private submission','approval notification','artist city category list','multi-view point relation','independent route','event venue','community membership','market binding','public listing comment and delete','private Book','selected share revoke','single doll outfit persistence','publisher controlled listing'],passed:true},null,2));
 }finally{if(server){server.closeAllConnections();await new Promise(r=>server.close(r));}await pool.end();}
});

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import {createRequire} from 'node:module';
import {serveStatic} from '../src/static.mjs';
const {chromium}=createRequire('D:/AI-Data/Cache/codex-runtimes/codex-primary-runtime/dependencies/node/entry.js')('playwright');
const artistId='11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111',id='22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222';
const community={id,artist_id:artistId,name:'安溥同路人'};

let memberships=[],writes=0,authError='',readFailure=false,writeFailure=false,release=null,hold=false;
const server=http.createServer(async(req,res)=>{
 const url=new URL(req.url,'http://localhost');if(await serveStatic(req,res,url))return;
 const reply=(status,value)=>{res.writeHead(status,{'content-type':'application/json'});res.end(JSON.stringify(value));};
 if(url.pathname==='/api/public')return reply(200,catalog);
 if(url.pathname.startsWith('/api/me/')){
  if(authError)return reply(authError==='AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED'?503:401,{error:authError});
  if(url.pathname.endsWith('/memberships'))return reply(readFailure?503:200,readFailure?{error:'UNAVAILABLE'}:memberships);
  return reply(200,url.pathname.endsWith('/profile')?{nickname:'测试用户'}:[]);
 }
 if(url.pathname==='/api/commands/community.join'){
  const chunks=[];for await(const chunk of req)chunks.push(chunk);const v=JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks));writes++;
  assert.equal(v.id,id);assert.ok(['joined','removed'].includes(v.status));assert.ok(req.headers['idempotency-key']);
  if(hold)await new Promise(resolve=>{release=resolve;});
  if(writeFailure)return reply(503,{error:'UNAVAILABLE'});
  memberships=v.status==='joined'?[{community_id:id,artist_id:artistId,status:'joined'}]:[];
  return reply(200,{id,status:v.status});
 }
 reply(404,{error:'NOT_FOUND'});
});
await new Promise(resolve=>server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1',resolve));
const base='http://127.0.0.1:'+server.address().port;

const join=()=>page.getByRole('button',{name:'加入社群',exact:true});
const leave=()=>page.getByRole('button',{name:'退出社群',exact:true});

 hold=true;await join().evaluate(b=>{b.click();b.click();});
 await page.waitForFunction(()=>document.querySelector('[data-membership-action]')?.disabled);
 while(!release)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,10));assert.equal(writes,1);hold=false;release();release=null;
 await leave().waitFor();checks.push('join suppresses duplicate writes and confirms server result');
 await page.reload();await leave().waitFor();checks.push('reload retains joined state');
 await open('#/community');await page.getByRole('region',{name:'我的社群'}).getByRole('link',{name:/安溥同路人/}).waitFor();checks.push('community overview reads joined communities');
 await open('#/me');await page.getByRole('link',{name:/安溥同路人/}).click();await leave().waitFor();checks.push('Me links back to joined community');

 writeFailure=false;await leave().click();await join().waitFor();
 await open('#/me');await page.getByText('还没有加入社群',{exact:true}).waitFor();checks.push('leave removes membership from Me');

import http from 'node:http';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import {createRequire} from 'node:module';
import {serveStatic} from '../src/static.mjs';
const {chromium}=createRequire('D:/AI-Data/Cache/codex-runtimes/codex-primary-runtime/dependencies/node/entry.js')('playwright');
const artist='11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111',community='22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222',listing='33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333';
const catalog={artists:[{id:artist,name:'安溥'}],communities:[{id:community,artist_id:artist,name:'安溥同路人'}],places:[],routes:[],events:[],listings:[]};
let own=[],comments=[],exchanges=[],auth=true,fail=false,writes=[];
const server=http.createServer(async(req,res)=>{const u=new URL(req.url,'http://localhost');if(await serveStatic(req,res,u))return;const reply=(code,data)=>{res.writeHead(code,{'content-type':'application/json'});res.end(JSON.stringify(data));};
 if(u.pathname==='/api/public')return reply(200,catalog);
 if(u.pathname.startsWith('/api/public/listing/'))return reply(200,{...catalog.listings[0],comments});
 if(u.pathname.startsWith('/api/me/'))return auth?reply(200,({listings:own,exchanges,listingComments:comments.map(c=>({id:c.id})),memberships:[{community_id:community,status:'joined'}]})[u.pathname.split('/').pop()]||[]):reply(503,{error:'AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED'});
 if(u.pathname.startsWith('/api/commands/')){const chunks=[];for await(const c of req)chunks.push(c);const v=JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks)),op=u.pathname.split('/').pop();writes.push({op,v});if(fail)return reply(503,{error:'UNAVAILABLE'});
 if(op==='listing.create'){own=[{id:listing,data:v,status:'available'}];catalog.listings=[{id:listing,...v}];}
 if(op==='listing.edit'){Object.assign(own[0].data,v);Object.assign(catalog.listings[0],v);}
 if(op==='listing.status')own[0].status=v.status;
 if(op==='listing.comment')comments.push({id:'comment',text:v.text,author:'测试用户'});
 if(op==='listing.comment.delete')comments=[];

================================================================================
FILE: deliveries/START_LOCAL_PLATFORM_2026-10-08/tests/module-loading.test.mjs
================================================================================
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {serveStatic} from '../src/static.mjs';

test('every browser module import is served at its resolved URL', async () => {
  const pending = [new URL('http://localhost/app/public.js')], visited = new Set();
  while (pending.length) {
    const url = pending.shift();
    if (visited.has(url.href)) continue;
    visited.add(url.href);
    let body, status, headers;
    const served = await serveStatic({method:'GET'}, {
      writeHead(code, values) { status=code; headers=values; },
      end(bytes) { body=String(bytes); },
    }, url);
    assert.equal(served,true,`Missing browser module: ${url.pathname}`);
    assert.equal(status,200);
    assert.match(headers['content-type'],/javascript/);
    for (const match of body.matchAll(/\bfrom\s+['"]([^'"]+)['"]/g)) {
      pending.push(new URL(match[1],url));
    }
  }
  assert.ok(visited.size>=3,'entry point, client and private views must all load');
});

 await denied(()=>call(admin,'submission.review',{id:pending.id,decision:'approve',checked:true}),'INVALID_INPUT');
 assert.equal(Number((await pool.query('SELECT count(*) FROM start_app.submissions')).rows[0].count),before);
});
test('failed route approval rolls back newly created stops and keeps submission pending',async()=>{
 const pending=await call(c,'routeSubmission.create',{name:'事务回滚路线',city:'济南',artistId:artist.id,stops:[{place:{name:'回滚站点',city:'济南',category:'其他',source:'测试',poiId:'ROLLBACK_STOP',longitude:117,latitude:36,coordinateSystem:'gcj02'}},{placeId:place.id}],source:'测试'});
 await call(admin,'content.withdraw',{id:place.id});
 await denied(()=>call(admin,'submission.review',{id:pending.id,decision:'approve',checked:true}),'NOT_FOUND');
 assert.equal(Number((await pool.query("SELECT count(*) FROM start_app.content WHERE poi_id='ROLLBACK_STOP'")).rows[0].count),0);
 assert.equal((await pool.query('SELECT status FROM start_app.submissions WHERE id=$1',[pending.id])).rows[0].status,'pending');
 const version=(await pool.query('SELECT version FROM start_app.content WHERE id=$1',[place.id])).rows[0].version;await call(admin,'content.publish',{id:place.id,version});
});
test('user corrections require review and homepage configuration accepts only public IDs',async()=>{
 const s=await call(c,'correction.create',{id:event.id,version:2,kind:'event',data:{name:'更正演出',city:'济南',artistIds:[artist.id],placeId:place.id,startsAt:'2026-10-11T12:00:00Z',timeZone:'Asia/Shanghai',status:'upcoming',source:'官方新公告'}});

 assert.equal((await app.publicDetail('event',event.id)).name,'更正演出');
 await denied(()=>call(admin,'home.configure',{ids:[randomUUID()]}),'NOT_FOUND');
 await call(admin,'home.configure',{ids:[event.id,place.id]});assert.equal((await app.home()).featured[0].id,event.id);
});

 const publicPlacesBeforeDeletion=(await app.publicQuery()).places.length;

 assert.equal((await app.publicQuery()).places.length,publicPlacesBeforeDeletion);
 assert.equal((await app.publicQuery()).communities[0].manager_id,null);
});

const server=http.createServer(async(req,res)=>{
  const url=new URL(req.url,'http://localhost');
  if(await serveStatic(req,res,url))return;
  let result;
  if(url.pathname==='/api/public')result=catalog;
  else if(url.pathname==='/api/extract/places')result={items:[{name:'测试咖啡',city:'上海'},{name:'原文不存在的地点',city:'上海'}]};
  else if(url.pathname.startsWith('/api/me/'))result=sections[url.pathname.split('/').pop()]??[];
  else if(url.pathname.startsWith('/api/commands/')){
    const chunks=[];for await(const chunk of req)chunks.push(chunk);
    commands.push({operation:url.pathname.split('/').pop(),value:JSON.parse(Buffer.concat(chunks))});
    await new Promise(resolve=>{release=resolve;});
    res.writeHead(failNext?503:200,{'content-type':'application/json'});
    res.end(JSON.stringify(failNext?{error:'AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED'}:{id:'test-only'}));return;
  }else{res.writeHead(404);res.end();return;}
  res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':'application/json'});res.end(JSON.stringify(result));
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {renderPrivateMe, renderSubmissionForm} from '../public-ui/private-views.mjs';
import fs from 'node:fs';

const publicScript = fs.readFileSync(new URL('../public-ui/public.js', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
const staticScript = fs.readFileSync(new URL('../src/static.mjs', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
const indexHtml = fs.readFileSync(new URL('../public-ui/index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');

test('private workspace exposes clear sections and auth boundary', () => {
  const html = renderPrivateMe({
    authError: 'AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED',
    profile: null,
    memberships: [],
    notifications: [],
    submissions: [],
    exchanges: [],
    listings: [],
    book: [],
    doll: null,
  });
  assert.match(html, /我的工作台/);
  assert.match(html, /我的投稿/);
  assert.match(html, /我的娃娃/);
  assert.match(html, /身份服务尚未接入/);
  assert.match(html, /提交地点或路线/);
});

test('submission form distinguishes place and route and gives labelled fields', () => {
  const place = renderSubmissionForm({kind: 'place', artists: [{id: 'artist-1', name: '安溥'}]});
  const route = renderSubmissionForm({kind: 'route', artists: [{id: 'artist-1', name: '安溥'}]});
  assert.match(place, /提交地点/);
  assert.match(route, /提交路线/);
  assert.match(place, /for="submission-name"/);
  assert.match(place, /地图搜索/);
  assert.match(place, /文字识别/);
  assert.match(route, /站点/);
});

test('private view is wired into the public shell and static allowlist', () => {
  assert.match(publicScript, /private-views\.mjs/);
  assert.match(publicScript, /s\.path==='\/submit'/);
  assert.match(staticScript, /\(css\|mjs\)/);
  assert.match(indexHtml, /private\.css/);
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {parse} from '../src/contracts.mjs';
const artistId='11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111',placeId='22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222';
const base={name:'上海路线',city:'上海',artistId,source:'用户核对来源'};
const place={name:'新咖啡店',city:'上海',category:'咖啡',source:'原帖',poiId:'B_NEW',longitude:121.4,latitude:31.2,coordinateSystem:'gcj02',sameSpot:'MV机位'};
test('route submission accepts ordered existing and map-selected new stops',()=>{
 assert.deepEqual(parse('routeSubmission.create',{...base,stops:[{placeId},{place}]}).stops,[{placeId},{place}]);
});
test('route rejects ambiguous stop sources, duplicate POIs and incomplete coordinates',()=>{
 for(const value of [
  {...base,stops:[]},
  {...base,placeIds:[placeId],stops:[{placeId}]},
  {...base,stops:[{placeId,place}]},
  {...base,stops:[{place},{place}]},
  {...base,stops:[{place:{...place,latitude:undefined}}]},
 ])assert.throws(()=>parse('routeSubmission.create',value),e=>e.code==='INVALID_INPUT');
});

function go(view){if(state.dirty){openDialog('离开编辑页',`<p>当前演示草稿尚未保存。</p><div class="dialog-actions">${button('继续编辑','close','secondary')}${button('丢弃本次编辑','discard','quiet',`data-next="${view}"`)}</div>`);return;}if(location.hash.slice(1)===view)fromHash();else location.hash=view;}
function fromHash(){const v=location.hash.slice(1);state.view=['library','place','route','event','media','review'].includes(v)?v:'library';state.dirty=false;if(state.view==='place')state.draft=clone(places.find(p=>p.id===state.recordId)||places[0]);else if(state.view==='route')state.draft=clone(additionalRecords.find(r=>r.id===state.recordId&&r.kind==='路线')||routeRecord);else if(state.view==='event')state.draft=clone(additionalRecords.find(r=>r.id===state.recordId&&r.kind==='演出')||eventRecord);else state.draft=null;render();}
function renderNav(){navEl.innerHTML=[['library','内容库','library'],['place','地点编辑','pin'],['route','路线编辑','route'],['event','演出编辑','event'],['media','素材库','media'],['review','投稿审核','review']].map(([v,n,i])=>`<button data-nav="${v}" class="${state.view===v?'selected':''}" aria-current="${state.view===v?'page':'false'}">${icon(i)}${n}</button>`).join('')+'<div class="later">后续编辑模块</div>'+[['artist','艺人资料'],['community','社群管理'],['library','首页内容配置']].map(([i,n])=>`<button data-later="${n}">${icon(i)}${n}</button>`).join('');}

function renderCatalog(){const items=[...places,routeRecord,eventRecord,...additionalRecords].filter(r=>(state.type==='全部'||r.kind===state.type)&&(state.status==='全部状态'||r.status===state.status)&&(!state.query||[r.name,r.city,r.artist].join(' ').includes(state.query)));document.querySelector('#row-count').textContent=items.length+' 条示例';document.querySelector('#catalog-results').innerHTML=items.length?items.map(r=>`<article class="catalog-row"><div class="content-name">${r.kind==='地点'?visual(r.cover):`<div class="thumb">${icon(r.kind==='路线'?'route':'event')}</div>`}<div><h3>${esc(r.name)}</h3><p>${r.kind} · ${r.kind==='演出'?'界面示例':'用户参考资料'}</p></div></div><span class="cell">${esc(r.artist)}</span><span class="cell hide-tablet">${esc(r.city||'待录入')}</span>${statusPill(r.status)}<button class="row-edit" data-edit="${r.id}" data-kind="${r.kind}">编辑</button></article>`).join(''):`<div class="empty"><h3>没有匹配内容</h3><p>试试其他名称或清除筛选。</p><div class="actions">${button('清除筛选','clear-filter','primary')}</div></div>`;}
